Surah: 98. Follow the brief below (surah_map.md) exactly. The evidence is text.md (the surah), dictionary.md (every root of the surah's words, each branch with the classical dictionaries' own phrases), channels.md and hft.md (both are earlier readers' proposals: ignore their judgements (grades, strength or confidence labels, reading types, statements of what a reading may or may not do) and make your own) and your own knowledge of Arabic and the Quran. Return only the map as your final message.

===== _commentary/v16/prompts/r2/surah_map.md =====
Read the surah as one text and write its map of image chains: the lexical images that run through several of
its ayat and join them into one scene, process or movement. A later writer will read one ayah at a time, with
only that ayah's own dictionary. The map lets that writer hear what the ayah's words carry in the surah as a
whole, including senses whose evidence sits under the words of other ayat.

Your evidence is the surah text, the dictionary of every root in the surah (each branch with the classical
dictionaries' own phrases), earlier readers' channel review (channels.md) and activation hypotheses (hft.md),
and your own knowledge of Arabic and the Quran. Where a member or passage comes from memory rather than from
the dictionary or the text, say so. Do not use tools, delegate, browse or inspect files.

The channel review and the HFT records are proposals by earlier readers. Ignore their judgements: grades,
strength or confidence labels, reading types, words such as "surprising", "exploratory" or "latent", and every
statement of what a reading may or may not do. Make your own judgement from the surah's words, the dictionary
phrases and the Quran. Do not rediscover what they already assembled: start from their chains, test each one
against the dictionary phrases and the text, and join, extend, split or correct them. Where their wording
abstracts a member, go back to the dictionary phrase and name what it actually says.

A chain belongs on the map when its members are senses the dictionary attests for words that stand in the
surah, and together they make one image or process that the surah's wording or sequence lets a listener hear.
A member need not be the sense that translates its word in its own ayah; a chain is heard across the surah,
not in one word. Say plainly when a member is a rare sense or is attested only inside a fixed expression, and
keep it. When the dictionary itself joins two of the surah's words in one phrase, quote that phrase: it is
the strongest evidence a chain can have. Keep a scene at the level of its objects, their parts and their
operation. When proposals share members, do not fold one into another's more abstract function unless nothing
concrete is lost. Carry every chain that meets this test, however unusual; leave out proposals that do not.

Write the map in English, with Arabic quoted exactly (surah wording from the text, dictionary phrases from the
dictionary). It is working material for the writer, not commentary prose.

1. `## Chains`. For each chain, a `###` heading naming the image. One paragraph on what the image is and how it
   moves through the surah. Then its members, one line each: the ayah ref, the word as it stands in the text,
   the root and branch id, the dictionary's own phrase quoted exactly, and what this member contributes to the
   image. Add Quran passages outside the surah, with exact refs, where they stage or confirm the chain.
2. `## Ayat`. For each ayah in order: which chains it opens, advances or completes, and what its words add to
   each.
3. `## Not carried`. Each channel subchannel and each HFT record you did not carry into a chain, one line each:
   its name and why.

No ranking and no labels of strength or confidence. No list of what a writer must include. There is no length
target and no required number of chains or members.


===== _commentary/v16/work/s098/surah.r2.hftbundle/text.md =====
# Surah 98

- 98:1 لَمْ يَكُنِ ٱلَّذِينَ كَفَرُوا۟ مِنْ أَهْلِ ٱلْكِتَٰبِ وَٱلْمُشْرِكِينَ مُنفَكِّينَ حَتَّىٰ تَأْتِيَهُمُ ٱلْبَيِّنَةُ
- 98:2 رَسُولٌۭ مِّنَ ٱللَّهِ يَتْلُوا۟ صُحُفًۭا مُّطَهَّرَةًۭ
- 98:3 فِيهَا كُتُبٌۭ قَيِّمَةٌۭ
- 98:4 وَمَا تَفَرَّقَ ٱلَّذِينَ أُوتُوا۟ ٱلْكِتَٰبَ إِلَّا مِنۢ بَعْدِ مَا جَآءَتْهُمُ ٱلْبَيِّنَةُ
- 98:5 وَمَآ أُمِرُوٓا۟ إِلَّا لِيَعْبُدُوا۟ ٱللَّهَ مُخْلِصِينَ لَهُ ٱلدِّينَ حُنَفَآءَ وَيُقِيمُوا۟ ٱلصَّلَوٰةَ وَيُؤْتُوا۟ ٱلزَّكَوٰةَ ۚ وَذَٰلِكَ دِينُ ٱلْقَيِّمَةِ
- 98:6 إِنَّ ٱلَّذِينَ كَفَرُوا۟ مِنْ أَهْلِ ٱلْكِتَٰبِ وَٱلْمُشْرِكِينَ فِى نَارِ جَهَنَّمَ خَٰلِدِينَ فِيهَآ ۚ أُو۟لَٰٓئِكَ هُمْ شَرُّ ٱلْبَرِيَّةِ
- 98:7 إِنَّ ٱلَّذِينَ ءَامَنُوا۟ وَعَمِلُوا۟ ٱلصَّٰلِحَٰتِ أُو۟لَٰٓئِكَ هُمْ خَيْرُ ٱلْبَرِيَّةِ
- 98:8 جَزَآؤُهُمْ عِندَ رَبِّهِمْ جَنَّٰتُ عَدْنٍۢ تَجْرِى مِن تَحْتِهَا ٱلْأَنْهَٰرُ خَٰلِدِينَ فِيهَآ أَبَدًۭا ۖ رَّضِىَ ٱللَّهُ عَنْهُمْ وَرَضُوا۟ عَنْهُ ۚ ذَٰلِكَ لِمَنْ خَشِىَ رَبَّهُۥ


===== _commentary/v16/work/s098/surah.r2.hftbundle/dictionary.md =====
# Dictionary: every branch of every focus root

Every root of the surah's words, each once, headed by the ayah and word where it first occurs; it also serves the later words listed with it.
One entry per branch: a Turkish label and Turkish glosses of its attested senses, then the classical dictionaries' own phrases with their source tags (ayn, sihah, tahdhib, maqayis, mufradat, jamhara). [kalıp] marks a sense the dictionaries attest only inside a fixed expression.
Identity roots come from the quran-data gateway. Word-scoped alternatives are cited analyses of this
exact word. **Echo roots** are observed but withheld mappings: sound-family candidates, not identity.

  الفرقان كتاب الله تعالى فرق به بين الحق والباطل (maqayis)؛ الفرقان كل كتاب أنزل به فرق الله بين الحق والباطل (ayn)؛ يجعل لكم فرقانا أي حجة ظاهرة وظفرا (ayn)؛ كل ما فرق به بين الحق والباطل فهو فرقان (sihah)؛ سمى الله الكتاب المنزل على محمد فرقانا وسمى الكتاب المنزل على موسى فرقانا (tahdhib)؛ يوم الفرقان هو يوم بدر (tahdhib;mufradat)؛ الفرقان أبلغ من الفرق لأنه يستعمل في الفرق بين الحق والباطل (mufradat)؛ نورا وتوفيقا على قلوبكم يفرق به بين الحق والباطل (mufradat)

  جاء فلان في أربية قومه، أي في أهل بيته من بني الأعمام ونحوهم، ولا تكون الأربية من غيرهم (sihah)

## ج ن ن (root_000266): 98:8 جَنَّٰتُ

- **B001** örtme ve duyulardan gizleme — örtmek; gizleyecek bir örtü sağlamak; içinde saklamak · bir şeyin arkasına gizlenmek · insanı örten giysi veya örtü
  الجيم والنون أصل واحد وهو الستر والتستر (maqayis)؛ أصل الجن ستر الشيء عن الحاسة (mufradat)؛ استجن فلان إذا استتر بشيء (ayn;tahdhib)؛ أجننت الشيء في صدري أكننته (sihah)؛ ما علي جنان إلا ما ترى أي ثوب يواريني (sihah;tahdhib)
- **B002** gecenin karartıp örtmesi — gecenin kararıp üzerini örtmesi · gecenin koyu karanlığı ve nesneleri örtmesi
  جنان الليل سواده وستره الأشياء (maqayis)؛ أجنه الليل وجن عليه الليل إذا أظلم حتى يستره بظلمته (ayn)؛ جن عليه الليل يجن بالضم جنونا (sihah)؛ جن عليه الليل وأجنه الليل إذا أظلم حتى يستره بظلمته (tahdhib)؛ جنه الليل وأجنه وجن عليه (mufradat)
- **B003** zemini ağaçlarla örtülü bahçe — zemini ağaçlarla örtülü bahçe veya koruluk
  الجنة البستان (maqayis;sihah)؛ الجنة الحديقة وهي بستان ذات شجر ونزهة (ayn)؛ العرب تسمي النخيل جنة (sihah)؛ كل بستان ذي شجر يستر بأشجاره الأرض (mufradat)
- **B004** ölüm sonrası gizli nimetler yurdu — ölüm sonrası ödül ve gizli nimetler yurdu
  الجنة ما يصير إليه المسلمون في الآخرة وهو ثواب مستور عنهم اليوم (maqayis)؛ سميت الجنة إما تشبيها بالجنة في الأرض وإما لستره نعمها عنا (mufradat)
- **B005** gözle görülmeyen ruhani varlıklar topluluğu — gözle görülmeyen ruhani varlıklar · görünmeyen varlıkların atası veya bir bireyi · görünmeyen ruhani varlıkların topluluğu · görünmeyen ruhani varlıkların çok bulunduğu yer
  الجن سموا بذلك لأنهم متسترون عن أعين الخلق (maqayis)؛ الجن جماعة ولد الجان وجمعهم الجنة والجنان (ayn;tahdhib)؛ الجن خلاف الإنس والواحد جني (sihah)؛ الجنة جماعة الجن (mufradat)؛ أرض مجنة كثيرة الجن (ayn;sihah;tahdhib)
- **B006** aklı örten akıl yitimi — aklını yitirmek; aklını yitirmiş duruma getirmek · akıl yitimi; benlik ile akıl arasındaki engel · aklını yitirmiş gibi davranmak
  الجنة الجنون وذلك أنه يغطي العقل (maqayis)؛ المجنة الجنون وجن الرجل وأجنه الله فهو مجنون (ayn)؛ جن الرجل جنونا وأجنه الله فهو مجنون (sihah)؛ به جنون وجنة ومجنة (tahdhib)؛ الجنون حائل بين النفس والعقل (mufradat)
- **B007** ana rahmindeki doğmamış çocuk — ana rahmindeki doğmamış çocuk · rahminde çocuk taşımak; çocuğun rahimde saklı kalması
  الجنين الولد في بطن أمه (maqayis)؛ أجنت الحامل الجنين أي الولد في بطنها (ayn)؛ الجنين الولد ما دام في البطن (sihah)؛ الجنين الولد في الرحم (tahdhib)؛ الجنين الولد ما دام في بطن أمه (mufradat)
- **B008** koruyucu siper veya savaş donanımı — koruyucu örtü, siper veya savaş donanımı · kalkan
  المجن الترس وكل ما استتر به من السلاح فهو جنة (maqayis)؛ المجن الترس والجنة الدرع وكل ما وقاك فهو جنتك (ayn)؛ الجنة ما استترت به من سلاح والجنة السترة والمجن الترس (sihah)؛ المجن الترس (tahdhib)؛ المجن والمجنة الترس الذي يجن صاحبه (mufradat)
- **B009** ölüyü örtüp gömme — ölüyü örtmek ve gömmek · gömüt; ölü örtüsü; gömülmüş kişi
  الجنين المقبور (maqayis)؛ الجنن القبر وقيل للكفن أيضا (ayn)؛ جننت الميت وأجننته أي واريته والجنن القبر (sihah)؛ جننته في القبر وأجننته والجنن القبر والجنن الكفن (tahdhib)؛ الجنين القبر (mufradat)
- **B010** duyulardan saklı yürek ve gizli yön — yürek veya yüreğin saklı iç yönü · gizli iş veya görünmeyen yön
  الجنان القلب (maqayis)؛ الجنان روع القلب (ayn;tahdhib)؛ أراد بالجن القلب (sihah)؛ الجنان القلب لكونه مستورا عن الحاسة (mufradat)؛ الجنان الأمر الخفي (tahdhib)
- **B011** bitkinin güçlenip boylanması ve sıklaşması — bitkinin güçlenmesi, boylanması, sıklaşması veya çiçek açması · uzun ağaç; bol otlu ve henüz otlanmamış arazi
  جن النبت جنونا إذا اشتد وخرج زهره (maqayis)؛ جن النبت جنونا أي طال والتف وخرج زهره ونخلة مجنونة أي طويلة (sihah)؛ للنبت الملتف الكثيف مجنون وجنت الرياض جنونا إذا اعتم نبتها (tahdhib)؛ جن التلاع والآفاق أي كثر عشبها (mufradat)
- **B012** yılan, özellikle beyaz bir tür — yılan, beyaz yılan veya belirli bir yılan türü
  الحية الذي يسمى الجان فهو تشبيه له بالواحد من الجان (maqayis)؛ الجان حية بيضاء (ayn)؛ الجان أيضا حية بيضاء (sihah)؛ الجان الحية وجمعها جوان (tahdhib)؛ الجان ضرب من الحيات (mufradat)
- **B013** halkın büyük kitlesi — insanların çoğunluğu veya halkın büyük kitlesi
  جنان الناس معظمهم ويسمى السواد (maqayis)؛ جنان الناس دهماؤهم (sihah)؛ جنانهم جماعتهم وسوادهم (tahdhib)
- **B014** bir şeyin ilk ve yeni dönemi — gençliğin, çocukluğun veya bir dönemin ilk başlangıcı
  كان ذلك في جن شبابه أي في أول شبابه (sihah)؛ كان ذلك في جن صباه أي في حداثته وكذلك جن كل شيء أول ابتدائه (tahdhib)
- **B015** uçuş sırasında çoğalan sinek vızıltısı — sineğin vızıltısının veya sesinin çoğalması · böcekse uçuş vızıltısının artması; bitkiyse sıklaşıp dolaşması
  جن الذباب أي كثر صوته (sihah)؛ جن الخازباز به جنونا يحتمل هذين الوجهين (sihah)؛ قيل هو ذباب وجنونه كثرة ترنمه في طيرانه وقيل هو نبت وجنون النبت التفافه (tahdhib)
- **B016** göğüs kemikleri ve kaburga uçları — göğüs kemikleri veya kaburgaların göğse yakın uçları
  الجناجن عظام الصدر (maqayis)؛ الجنجن والجناجن أطراف الأضلاع مما يلي الصدر وعظم القلب (ayn)؛ الجناجن عظام الصدر الواحد جنجن (sihah)
- **B017** içine girilip saklanılan yer — saklanılan yer; ayrıca kaynakta belirli bir eski pazar yerinin adı
  المجنة اسم موضع على أميال من مكة؛ كانت مجنة وذو المجاز وعكاظ أسواقا في الجاهلية؛ المجنة أيضا الموضع الذي يستتر فيه (sihah)

## ج ر ي (root_000240): 98:8 تَجْرِى

- **B001** bir yol boyunca akıp, koşup ya da ilerleyerek gitme — hızlı ilerleyiş ve kendi yatağında akış · aktı, koştu ya da yol aldı · suyun akışı · at koşusu · akıtmak ya da harekete geçirmek · akış, gidiş yolu ya da koşu yeri · denizde yol alan gemi · gökte yol alan güneş · suyu akan pınar · denizde yol alan gemiler · çeşitli koşu biçimleri olan at · kötücül ayartıcının sizi kendi işi doğrultusunda sürüklemesine ya da kendine aracı kılmasına izin vermeyin
  أصل واحد وهو انسياح الشيء (maqayis)؛ جرى الماء يجري جرية وجريا وجريانا (maqayis;sihah;mufradat)؛ الخيل تجري والرياح تجري والشمس تجري جريا (ayn;tahdhib)؛ الجارية السفينة والجارية الشمس (maqayis;sihah)
- **B002** alışılmış yol ve davranış düzeni — kişinin alışkanlık edindiği ve sürekli izlediği yol
  للعادة الإجريا (maqayis)؛ الإجريا طريقته التي يجري عليها من عادته (ayn)؛ الإجريا الجري والعادة مما تأخذ فيه (sihah)؛ الإجرياء الوجه الذي نأخذ فيه (tahdhib)؛ الإجريا العادة التي يجري عليها الإنسان (mufradat)
- **B003** başkası adına iş gören, haber götüren ya da güvence veren kimse — başkası adına iş gören, haber götüren ya da güvence veren kimse · başkası adına iş görecek birini tutmak · kötücül ayartıcının sizi kendi işi doğrultusunda sürüklemesine ya da kendine aracı kılmasına izin vermeyin
  الجري الوكيل (maqayis;tahdhib)؛ الجري الرسول (ayn;tahdhib;mufradat)؛ الجري الضامن (tahdhib)؛ استجريت أي اتخذت وكيلا (maqayis;sihah;tahdhib)؛ لا يستجرينكم الشيطان (maqayis;sihah;tahdhib;mufradat)
- **B004** genç kız ve ona bağlı genç kızlık çağı — genç kız ya da hizmette çalıştırılan genç kadın · genç kızlık çağı · genç kızlık durumu
  الجارية من النساء لأنها تستجرى في الخدمة (maqayis)؛ الجارية مصدرها الجراء (ayn)؛ جارية بينة الجراية والجراء (sihah;tahdhib)؛ أيام جرائها أي صباها (maqayis;ayn;sihah)
- **B005** kuş kursağı — 
  الجرية وهي الحوصلة أصلها قرية (maqayis)؛ الجرية مثل القرية هي الحوصلة (sihah)؛ الجرية والقرية والنوطة لحوصلة الطائر (tahdhib)؛ يقال للحوصلة جرية لأنها مجرى الطعام (mufradat)
- **B006** sürekli verilen geçimlik ya da kalıcı yarar — düzenli görev ödeneği · onun için sürekli verildi ya da sürüp gitti · ona sürekli olarak verdim · yararı süren bağış
  الجراية الجاري من الوظائف (sihah)؛ الأرزاق جارية والأعطيات دارة (tahdhib)؛ جرى عليه ذلك الشيء ودر له بمعنى دام له (tahdhib)؛ أجريت له كذا أي أدمت له (tahdhib)؛ صدقة جارية (tahdhib)
- **B007** birlikte ilerleyip birbirine ayak uydurma — yanında koşmak ya da ona ayak uydurmak · söyleşide ona ayak uydurmak ve karşılık vermek
  جاراه مجاراة وجراء أي جرى معه (sihah)؛ جاراه في الحديث وتجاروا فيه (sihah)
- **B008** senin yüzünden ya da senin için — senin yüzünden ya da senin için

## ن ه ر (root_001559): 98:8 ٱلْأَنْهَٰرُ

- **B001** bol su taşıyan doğal akarsu yatağı — taşkın suyun aktığı doğal akarsu yatağı · akarsu yatakları · akarsular veya akarsu yatakları · akarsu yatağı sağlam bir güzergah edindi · su aktı ve kendine bir yatak açtı · bol sulu veya geniş akarsu · suyun kazıp açtığı yatak yeri · kuyu kazısı suya ulaştı
  النهر مجرى الماء الفائض (mufradat)؛ النهر واحد الأنهار وجمعه أنهار ونهر (ayn;sihah;tahdhib;maqayis)؛ سمي النهر لأنه ينهر الأرض أي يشقها (maqayis)؛ استنهر النهر أخذ مجراه (ayn;tahdhib;maqayis)؛ نهر الماء أو أنهر الماء جرى (sihah;tahdhib;maqayis)؛ نهر نهر كثير الماء (sihah;tahdhib;maqayis;mufradat)؛ حفرت البئر حتى نهرت أي بلغت الماء (tahdhib)
- **B002** şafaktan gün batımına aydınlık gündüz — şafaktan güneş batımına kadar süren aydınlık gündüz · aydınlık gündüz süreleri · gündüz vaktinde baskın yapan kimse · gündüzün aydınlığına girdik
  النهار ضياء ما بين طلوع الفجر إلى غروب الشمس (ayn;tahdhib;maqayis)؛ النهار ضد الليل (sihah)؛ الوقت الذي ينتشر فيه الضوء (mufradat)؛ النهار اسم لكل يوم (tahdhib)؛ النهار يجمع على نهر (sihah;tahdhib;maqayis)؛ رجل نهر صاحب نهار (ayn;sihah;tahdhib;maqayis;mufradat)
- **B003** bir şeyi açma veya genişletme — genişlik veya aydınlıkla birlikte genişlik · kanı açıp serbest bırakarak akıttı · yaranın veya yarığın açıklığını genişletti · genişledi · evlerin önleri arasında atık bırakılan açık alan · bağırsağı çözüldü ve akarsu gibi boşaldı · tehlikeler; birleşik bir türetme olarak açıklanan biçim
  أصل صحيح يدل على تفتح شيء أو فتحه (maqayis)؛ أنهرت الدم فتحته وأرسلته (maqayis)؛ أنهرت الدم أي أسلته (sihah;mufradat)؛ أنهرت الطعنة وسعتها وأنهر فتقها (sihah;tahdhib)؛ نهر من نهر الفتق (maqayis)؛ استنهر الشيء اتسع (sihah)؛ المنهرة فضاء يكون بين أفنية القوم (maqayis;sihah;mufradat)؛ أنهر بطنه إذا جاء بطنه مثل مجيء النهر (tahdhib)؛ النهر السعة تشبيها بنهر الماء وفي ضياء وسعة (mufradat;sihah;tahdhib)
- **B004** sert sözle azarlayıp engelleme [kalıp] — onu sert sözle azarlayıp engelledi
  نهرت الرجل نهرا وانتهرته انتهارا زجرته بكلام عن شر (ayn)؛ نهره وانتهره أي زبره (sihah)؛ نهرته وانتهرته إذا استقبلته بكلام تزجره (tahdhib)؛ النهر والانتهار الزجر بمغالظة (mufradat)
- **B005** bazı kuşların yavrusu — türü aktarıma göre değişen bir kuş yavrusu
  النهار فرخ القطا والغطاط والعقاب ونحوه وثلاثة أنهرة (ayn)؛ النهار فرخ الحبارى (sihah;tahdhib;mufradat)؛ النهار فرخ بعض الطير مما لا يعرج على مثله ولا معنى له (maqayis)
- **B006** fırsat kollayıp gizlice kapma — fırsat kollayıp ani biçimde gizlice kapma
  النهر الدغرة وهي الخلسة (tahdhib)
- **B007** kişi, yer ve yıldızlara ait özel adlar — belirli bir topluluktan bir şairin adı · bir yer adı · sularının bolluğu nedeniyle iki yıldız için kullanılan ortak ad
  نهار بن توسعة اسم شاعر من تميم (sihah)؛ نهروان بلد (sihah)؛ العرب تسمي العواء والسماك الأنهرين لكثرة مائهما (tahdhib)
- **B008** bulut — bulut

## ء ب د (root_000004): 98:8 أَبَدًا

- **B001** sonsuz süre ve kalıcı kılma — sonsuz zaman, çok uzun süre · sonsuza dek, kesintisiz olarak · çağlar boyunca, sonsuza dek · bütün zaman boyunca · kalıcı kılma · sürekli kılınmış, devredilemez · sonsuza dek veya çok uzun süre kalmak
  طول المدة (maqayis)؛ الأبد الدهر (maqayis;sihah)؛ أبد الآبدين وأبد الدهر (tahdhib)؛ الأبد الدائم والتأبيد التخليد (sihah)؛ مدة الزمان الممتد (mufradat)؛ وقفا مؤبدا وتأبيدا (tahdhib)
- **B002** yabanıllaşıp insandan ürkme — devenin yabanıllaşması · hayvanın yabanıllaşıp insandan ürkmesi · yabani ve ürkek hayvanlar · yabani inek
  تأبد البعير توحش (maqayis;mufradat)؛ أوابد كأوابد الوحش (maqayis;tahdhib)؛ أبدت البهيمة أي توحشت (sihah)؛ توحشت ونفرت من الإنس (tahdhib)؛ الوحشيات (mufradat)
- **B003** terk edilip ıssızlaşmak [kalıp] — evin terk edilip ıssızlaşması ve yabani hayvanlara kalması

- **B004** her yıl doğuran dişi — her yıl doğuran dişi eşek, kısrak veya köle kadın
  الإبد ذات النتاج من المال كالأمة والفرس والأتان (maqayis)؛ الابد الولود من أمة أو أتان (sihah)؛ أتان إبد في كل عام تلد (tahdhib)
- **B005** yüzün lekelenip sertleşmesi [kalıp] — yüzün lekelenmesi, sertleşmesi veya öfkeden değişmesi
  تأبد وجهه كلف (maqayis)؛ تأبد وجه فلان توحش وقد فسر بغضب (mufradat)
- **B006** bir yerde kalıp ayrılmama — bir yerde kalıp oradan ayrılmamak · kış yaz aynı arazide kalan kuşlar
  أبد بالمكان أي أقام به (sihah)؛ أبدت بالمكان إذا أقمت به ولم تبرحه (tahdhib)؛ الطير المقيمة بأرض شتاءها وصيفها أوابد (tahdhib)
- **B007** uzun süre anılan olağanüstü olay — uzun süre anılan olağanüstü iş veya olay · unutulmayacak kadar sıra dışı bir iş yapmak
  الأبدة الفعلة تبقى على الأبد (maqayis)؛ جاء فلان بآبدة أي بداهية يبقى ذكرها على الأبد (sihah)
- **B008** yadırgatıcı söz veya başıboş uyak — yadırgatıcı, alışılmadık sözcük · şiirin başıboş veya aykırı uyakları
  الشوارد من القوافي أوابد (sihah)؛ الكلمة الوحشية آبدة وجمعه الأوابد (tahdhib)
- **B009** öfkelenmek veya birine öfkelenmek [kalıp] — adamın öfkelenmesi · ona öfkelenmek
  أبد الرجل غضب (sihah)؛ أبد إذا غضب عليه (tahdhib)؛ وقد فسر بغضب (mufradat)

(source: latent_activation/network/v3/reviews/s098/reader_a_pilot.md)

# s098 Semantic Channel Discovery

## Parent Channels

### 1. Arrival, Disclosure, and Transmission
- Semantic invariant: Something remote, concealed, or merely potential crosses into presence and becomes communicable.
- Surface relation: direct; 98:1-4 center on تأتي, رسول, صحفا, كتب, جاءت, and البينة.
- Surprising reach: Arrival expands from revealed proof into route markers, answering voices, and failures of reading.

#### Subchannel A. Arriving Proof
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: A messenger-borne disclosure comes into a waiting community and makes what was hidden manifest.
- Active motifs: coming into presence `ء ت ي:B001/m01`; arrival `ج ي ء:B001/m01`; manifest proof `ب ي ن:B004/m01`; message bearer `ر س ل:B002/m01`; bringing forward `ج ي ء:B004/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:1, 98:4, 98:5 (تأتي/أوتوا/يؤتوا); 98:4 (جاءت); 98:1, 98:4 (البينة); 98:2 (رسول)
- Synthesis: The two coming-roots supply approach and achieved arrival, while the messenger carries the disclosed content and البينة names its unveiled state. Bringing is therefore both motion toward recipients and transfer into public intelligibility.

#### Subchannel B. Ordered, Purified Sheets
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Written sheets are arranged in a following sequence and presented in a purified state.
- Active motifs: ordered following `ت ل و:B001/m01`; written sheet `ص ح ف:B002/m01`; removal of defilement `ط ه ر:B001/m01`; carried message `ر س ل:B002/m02`
- Ayah anchors: 98:2 (رسول/يتلوا/صحفا/مطهرة)
- Synthesis: The surface recitation rests on a lexical image of one item following another. Pages, message, and purification form a controlled transmission scene in which both sequence and medium are made fit for reception.

#### Subchannel C. Signs, Waymarks, and Beacons
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A traveler or hearer is oriented by signs that reveal meaning, mark a route, or shine from a distance.
- Active motifs: explanatory sign `ب ي ن:B005/m01`; identifying sign `ء م ر:B005/m01`; road waymark `ء م ر:B005/m02`; beacon `ن و ر:B005/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:1, 98:4 (البينة); 98:5 (أمروا); 98:6 (نار)
- Synthesis: بيان makes meaning legible, أمارة fixes a recognizable marker, and the beacon extends recognition across distance. The surface proof thus resonates with practical navigation: disclosure is something by which a course can be found.

#### Subchannel D. Distorted Reading and False Attribution
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A written or spoken transmission is misread, converted into a false statement, and attached to someone as an accusation.
- Active motifs: textual misreading `ص ح ف:B005/m01`; false following statement `ت ل و:B009/m01`; unattributed report `ر س ل:B011/m03`; accusation of unbelief `ك ف ر:B006/m01`; deficient judgment `ف ك ك:B006/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:2 (رسول/يتلوا/صحفا); 98:1, 98:6 (كفروا); 98:1 (منفكين)
- Synthesis: The channel reverses orderly transmission. A page is read wrongly, the resulting utterance becomes false, and attribution hardens into accusation; lax judgment explains how the communicative chain loses integrity.

### 2. Written Form and Documentary Force
- Semantic invariant: Marks are joined into durable textual objects that can state, value, and bind.
- Surface relation: direct; 98:1-4 and 98:6 repeatedly invoke الكتاب, كتب, and صحفا.
- Surprising reach: The same lexical field moves from physical stitching to appraisal and enforceable decree.

#### Subchannel A. From Broad Sheet to Bound Codex
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A broad writable surface receives script, is joined at its edges, and becomes a collected book.
- Active motifs: broad surface `ص ح ف:B001/m01`; written page `ص ح ف:B002/m01`; gathered codex `ص ح ف:B003/m01`; stitching and joining `ك ت ب:B001/m01`; inscription `ك ت ب:B002/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:2 (صحفا); 98:1, 98:3, 98:4, 98:6 (الكتاب/كتب)
- Synthesis: The document is reconstructed materially: breadth provides the sheet, writing fills it, joining closes it, and collected sheets produce the codex. The surface books therefore retain a latent craft image of assembled textual bodies.

#### Subchannel B. Decree, Upright Record, and Appraisal
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A written statement acquires normative force and is assessed as straight, valuable, or authoritative.
- Active motifs: prescribed decree `ك ت ب:B003/m01`; standing value `ق و م:B010/m01`; straightness `ق و م:B008/m01`; written explanation `ب ي ن:B005/m02`
- Ayah anchors: 98:1, 98:3, 98:4, 98:6 (الكتاب/كتب); 98:3, 98:5 (قيمة/يقيموا/القيمة); 98:1, 98:4 (البينة)
- Synthesis: Writing moves beyond storage into prescription. The record stands because it is both normatively straight and open to appraisal, while بيان supplies the act by which its intended meaning is set out.

### 3. Devotion, Uprightness, and Purification
- Semantic invariant: A person is directed, straightened, and cleansed for undivided service.
- Surface relation: direct; 98:2 and 98:5 explicitly join الله, مطهرة, يعبدوا, مخلصين, حنفاء, الصلاة, and الزكاة.
- Surprising reach: Ritual posture extends into lowered submission, purification media, and places built for worship.

#### Subchannel A. Undivided Worship
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: Worship is directed to one deity through obedience and an allotment withheld from partners.
- Active motifs: deity and worship `ء ل ه:B001/m01`; worshipful service `ع ب د:B003/m01`; commanded obedience `ء م ر:B002/m01`; obedient religion `د ي ن:B001/m01`; unshared devotion `خ ل ص:B004/m01`; obedient disposition `ر ض و:B006/m01`; obedient disposition `ر ض ي:B004/m01`; partner-making `ش ر ك:B002/m01`; lowered submission `ك ف ر:B014/m01`; willing submission `ك و ن:B004/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:2, 98:5, 98:8 (الله); 98:5 (أمروا/يعبدوا/الدين/مخلصين); 98:8 (رضي/رضوا); 98:1, 98:6 (المشركين/كفروا); 98:1 (يكن)
- Synthesis: The central act is exclusive direction: service and دين are assigned without a partner. The latent image of bodily lowering intensifies this orientation, while partnership marks the precise relation being refused.

#### Subchannel B. Upright Prayer
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: A worshipper turns toward a chosen direction, stands straight, and establishes ritual prayer.
- Active motifs: directional inclination `ح ن ف:B002/m01`; standing posture `ق و م:B002/m01`; straight alignment `ق و م:B008/m01`; ritual prayer `ص ل و:B003/m01`; worship place `ص ل و:B007/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:5 (حنفاء/يقيموا/الصلاة/القيمة)
- Synthesis: Inclination is not aimless bending but directed reorientation. Standing and straightness convert that turn into stable posture, and prayer gives the posture its repeated ritual form and architectural setting.

#### Subchannel C. Purified Character and Righteous Work
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Defilement is removed from the self, growth is made clean, and intentional work repairs what is wrong.
- Active motifs: moral purification `ط ه ر:B005/m01`; purity and increase `ز ك و:B002/m01`; filtering refinement `خ ل ص:B001/m01`; repair and righteousness `ص ل ح:B001/m01`; intentional work `ع م ل:B001/m01`; moral good `خ ي ر:B001/m01`; expiation `ك ف ر:B009/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:2 (مطهرة); 98:5 (مخلصين/الزكاة); 98:7 (عملوا/الصالحات/خير); 98:1, 98:6 (كفروا)
- Synthesis: Cleansing, growth, repair, and work form one ethical process: contamination is removed, capacity develops, and the resulting action restores fitness. Expiation supplies the remedial counterpart when action has incurred a stain.

#### Subchannel D. Invocation, Amen, and Blessing
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A worshipper names the divine, addresses the lord, asks for blessing, and seals the utterance with amen.
- Active motifs: divine invocation `ء ل ه:B002/m01`; amen response `ء م ن:B003/m01`; supplication and blessing `ص ل و:B002/m01`; lordship addressed `ر ب ب:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:2, 98:5, 98:8 (الله); 98:7 (آمنوا); 98:5 (الصلاة); 98:8 (ربهم/ربه)
- Synthesis: Naming opens the address, lordship identifies the addressee, supplication carries the request, and amen supplies the answering assent. The result is a compact call-and-response ritual.

### 4. Conviction, Awe, and Acceptance
- Semantic invariant: Inner judgment settles into belief, guarded fear, evaluation, or contentment.
- Surface relation: direct; 98:7-8 join آمنوا, خير, رضي/رضوا, خشي, and ربهم.
- Surprising reach: Interior stance reaches learned discernment, animal skittishness, and negotiated satisfaction.

#### Subchannel A. Conviction and Learned Discernment
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A claim is inwardly assented to and interpreted through knowledgeable, delegated judgment.
- Active motifs: belief and assent `ء م ن:B002/m01`; knowledge carried by fear-language `خ ش ي:B002/m01`; learned religious guide `ر ب ب:B003/m01`; delegated adjudication `د ي ن:B007/m01`; denial `ك ف ر:B003/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:7 (آمنوا); 98:8 (خشي/ربه); 98:5 (الدين); 98:1, 98:6 (كفروا)
- Synthesis: Belief is presented as a settled cognitive act rather than mere safety. Learned interpretation and delegated judgment give it a disciplined frame, while denial provides the opposing refusal to assent.

#### Subchannel B. Security Held Beside Awe
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Trust establishes safety while reverent fear and alarm keep the subject alert.
- Active motifs: security and trust `ء م ن:B001/m01`; awe and apprehension `خ ش ي:B001/m01`; fear after separation `ف ر ق:B009/m01`; skittish aversion `ن و ر:B006/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:7 (آمنوا); 98:8 (خشي); 98:4 (تفرق); 98:6 (نار)
- Synthesis: Security and fear are not simple opposites here. Trust supplies a stable relation, while awe, alarm, and skittish recoil regulate approach to what carries authority or danger.

#### Subchannel C. Acceptance and Mutual Satisfaction
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: Approval moves from individual contentment to reciprocal satisfaction between parties.
- Active motifs: acceptance `ر ض و:B001/m01`; abundant satisfaction `ر ض و:B002/m01`; seeking approval `ر ض و:B004/m01`; accepted state `ر ض ي:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:8 (رضي/رضوا)
- Synthesis: The surface reciprocity is expanded into a process: satisfaction is sought, received, and made abundant. The two morphological branch sets converge on a relation in which approval travels in both directions.

#### Subchannel D. Selection, Fitness, and Moral Polarity
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Persons or actions are compared, chosen, and found fit against an opposing field of evil and ingratitude.
- Active motifs: excellence and selection `خ ي ر:B002/m01`; choosing `خ ي ر:B003/m01`; fitness `ص ل ح:B003/m01`; worthiness `ء ه ل:B003/m01`; compatibility `ز ك و:B004/m01`; evil `ش ر ر:B001/m01`; ingratitude `ك ف ر:B004/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:7 (خير/الصالحات); 98:1, 98:6 (أهل/كفروا); 98:5 (الزكاة); 98:6 (شر)
- Synthesis: Evaluation proceeds from comparison to selection and then to demonstrated fitness. Evil and ingratitude are not merely labels but the negative pole against which worthiness, compatibility, and excellence become visible.

### 5. Origination, Existence, and Return
- Semantic invariant: What does not yet exist is formed, sustained, and capable of being raised again.
- Surface relation: direct; 98:1 uses يكن, 98:6-7 use البرية, and 98:8 places recompensed creatures before their lord.
- Surprising reach: Creation and resurrection share an image of standing into renewed existence.

#### Subchannel A. Formation into Existence
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A created being comes into occurrence and is nurtured toward a repaired, viable state.
- Active motifs: creation and formation `ب ر ء:B001/m01`; occurrence and existence `ك و ن:B001/m01`; nurture and repair `ر ب ب:B002/m01`; activation `ع م ل:B002/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:6, 98:7 (البرية); 98:1 (يكن); 98:8 (ربهم/ربه); 98:7 (عملوا)
- Synthesis: Origination is not an isolated instant. Coming-to-be is followed by nurture, repair, and activation, producing a causal sequence from formation to functioning life.

#### Subchannel B. Rising Again
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Created beings stand into a renewed state whose duration can extend without end.
- Active motifs: resurrection and rising `ق و م:B013/m01`; renewed creation `ب ر ء:B001/m01`; lasting duration `ء ب د:B001/m01`; permanence `خ ل د:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:3, 98:5 (قيمة/يقيموا/القيمة); 98:6, 98:7 (البرية); 98:8 (أبدا); 98:6, 98:8 (خالدين)
- Synthesis: Standing supplies the motion of return, creation supplies the recreated subject, and the two duration roots extend the resulting state. The scene links beginning and afterlife through renewed existence.

### 6. Accounting, Debt, and Substitution
- Semantic invariant: An obligation is assessed and answered by recompense, payment, discharge, or a substitute.
- Surface relation: direct; 98:5 names الدين and 98:8 opens with جزاؤهم.
- Surprising reach: Eschatological recompense extends into debt collection, tribute, guarantors, and proxy performance.

#### Subchannel A. Judgment and Recompense
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: Conduct is judged under a written norm and answered with a corresponding outcome.
- Active motifs: judgment and accounting `د ي ن:B002/m01`; recompense `ج ز ي:B001/m01`; written decree `ك ت ب:B003/m01`; adjudicative standing `ق و م:B013/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:5 (الدين/دين); 98:8 (جزاؤهم); 98:1, 98:3, 98:4, 98:6 (الكتاب/كتب); 98:3, 98:5 (قيمة/يقيموا/القيمة)
- Synthesis: The norm is fixed, the subject stands before it, and judgment produces a matched return. This gives the surface reward an institutional grammar of decree, hearing, and result.

#### Subchannel B. Debt Collection and Discharge
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A claim remains attached until it is collected, fulfilled, or cleared from the debtor.
- Active motifs: debt `د ي ن:B003/m01`; collection of what is due `ج ز ي:B003/m01`; clearing a right or debt `ب ر ء:B004/m01`; attached liability `ت ل و:B004/m01`; demand to take `ع ن د:B006/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:5 (الدين/دين); 98:8 (جزاؤهم/عند); 98:6, 98:7 (البرية); 98:2 (يتلوا)
- Synthesis: Liability follows the obligated party until collection or release. The same accounting relation can therefore terminate either in payment or in a formal clearing of the outstanding right.

#### Subchannel C. Tax and Tribute
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: An authority levies a due, receives tribute, and marks the payer's subordinate position.
- Active motifs: assessed levy `ء ت ي:B008/m01`; tribute payment `ج ز ي:B004/m01`; lowered submission `ك ف ر:B014/m02`; debt obligation `د ي ن:B003/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:1, 98:4, 98:5 (تأتي/أوتوا/يؤتوا); 98:8 (جزاؤهم); 98:1, 98:6 (كفروا); 98:5 (الدين/دين)
- Synthesis: Giving becomes compulsory transfer: a due is assessed, collected as tribute, and embedded in a hierarchy. This is the fiscal counterpart of voluntary giving elsewhere in the surah's lexical field.

#### Subchannel D. Proxy, Sufficiency, and Guarantee
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: One agent stands in for another, performs the required role, or guarantees that the obligation will be met.
- Active motifs: substitution `ج ز ي:B002/m01`; sufficiency in another's place `ج ز ي:B002/m02`; substitute office `ق و م:B007/m01`; surety `ك و ن:B003/m01`; delegated agent `ج ر ي:B003/m01`; guarantor `ر ض و:B006/m03`; guarantor `ر ض ي:B004/m03`
- Ayah anchors: 98:8 (جزاؤهم/تجري/رضي/رضوا); 98:3, 98:5 (قيمة/يقيموا/القيمة); 98:1 (يكن)
- Synthesis: Substitution is treated as a functional relation: the proxy occupies a position, the delegate acts, and the guarantor secures completion. Sufficiency names the successful outcome of that replacement.

### 7. Authority, Office, and Civic Order
- Semantic invariant: Collective life is organized through offices, records, covenants, and ranked places.
- Surface relation: indirect; 98:5 supplies command and ordered practice, while 98:8 supplies lordship and station-like nearness.
- Surprising reach: The field reaches payrolls, civic enrollment, coronation, and maritime command.

#### Subchannel A. Authority and Stewardship
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: An office-holder receives authority, supervises affairs, and maintains a governed charge.
- Active motifs: authority and office `ء م ر:B003/m01`; stewardship `ق و م:B004/m01`; administrative work `ع م ل:B003/m01`; lordship `ر ب ب:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:5 (أمروا); 98:3, 98:5 (قيمة/يقيموا/القيمة); 98:7 (عملوا); 98:8 (ربهم/ربه)
- Synthesis: Command is institutionalized as office, work becomes administration, and standing becomes guardianship. Lordship supplies the highest form of the same relation: ordered care exercised over a charge.

#### Subchannel B. Enrollment, Roster, and Payroll
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Workers are entered into a register, assigned service, and maintained by wage or stipend.
- Active motifs: enrollment and roster `ك ت ب:B004/m01`; office work `ع م ل:B003/m01`; laboring crew `ع م ل:B006/m01`; wage `ع م ل:B004/m01`; continuing stipend `ج ر ي:B006/m01`; livelihood `ق و م:B009/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:1, 98:3, 98:4, 98:6 (الكتاب/كتب); 98:7 (عملوا); 98:8 (تجري); 98:3, 98:5 (قيمة/يقيموا/القيمة)
- Synthesis: The register fixes membership, office assigns function, and recurrent payment sustains the worker. Writing thus operates as a practical technology for coordinating labor and livelihood.

#### Subchannel C. Covenant and Treaty
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Parties bind themselves by covenant and stabilize the relation through mutual assent.
- Active motifs: covenant `ر ب ب:B011/m01`; communal party `ء ه ل:B001/m01`; partnership `ش ر ك:B001/m01`; mutual satisfaction `ر ض و:B003/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:8 (ربهم/ربه/رضي/رضوا); 98:1, 98:6 (أهل/المشركين)
- Synthesis: A covenant gives durable form to a relation among collective parties. Partnership defines shared interest, and reciprocal satisfaction supplies the condition under which the pact can hold.

#### Subchannel D. Civic Station, Chief, and Crown
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A settled polity is arranged around a station, a chief, and visible insignia of rule.
- Active motifs: city and polity `د ي ن:B006/m01`; established station `ق و م:B006/m01`; located place `ك و ن:B002/m01`; chief of sailors `ر ب ب:B017/m01`; covering crown `ك ف ر:B015/m01`; dignity and service `ع ب د:B006/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:5 (الدين/دين); 98:3, 98:5 (قيمة/يقيموا/القيمة); 98:1 (يكن); 98:8 (ربهم/ربه); 98:1, 98:6 (كفروا); 98:5 (يعبدوا)
- Synthesis: Place becomes civic station, station receives leadership, and the crown makes rank visible. The maritime chief extends the hierarchy into a mobile polity whose order must be maintained at sea.

### 8. Affiliation, Household, and Social Placement
- Semantic invariant: Persons are located within households, crowds, ranks, or the margins outside them.
- Surface relation: direct; 98:1 and 98:6 distinguish أهل الكتاب and المشركين, while 98:7-8 treat collective human outcomes.
- Surprising reach: Affiliation expands into stepfamilies, hospitality, distant kin, wild aversion, and underclass position.

#### Subchannel A. People, Crowd, and Faction
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Individuals gather into a people, a dense crowd, a faction, or a named group.
- Active motifs: affiliated people `ء ه ل:B001/m01`; people as collective `ق و م:B001/m01`; mass of people `ج ن ن:B013/m01`; large gathered companies `ر ب ب:B004/m01`; faction or flock `ف ر ق:B005/m01`; grouped companies `ع د ن:B005/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:1, 98:6 (أهل); 98:3, 98:5 (قيمة/يقيموا/القيمة); 98:8 (جنات/ربهم/عدن); 98:4 (تفرق)
- Synthesis: The channel ranges from affiliation by أهل to the visual density of a crowd. Faction and grouped companies show how a collective can be internally organized or split into bounded bodies.

#### Subchannel B. Marriage, Affinity, and Caregiving
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Marriage creates an affinity network containing spouses, stepchildren, caregivers, and loving obligation.
- Active motifs: marriage and household `ء ه ل:B002/m01`; in-law affinity `ش ر ك:B003/m01`; stepchild `ر ب ب:B005/m01`; caregiver `ر ب ب:B005/m02`; loving relation `ر ض و:B006/m02`; loving relation `ر ض ي:B004/m02`
- Ayah anchors: 98:1, 98:6 (أهل/المشركين); 98:8 (ربهم/ربه/رضي/رضوا)
- Synthesis: Partnership is recast as kinship, and lordly nurture contracts into the intimate work of raising a child from an earlier union. Love gives the resulting household relation an affective rather than merely legal bond.

#### Subchannel C. Hospitality and Familiar Settlement
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: An inhabited place receives a newcomer and turns unfamiliar presence into welcome and calm.
- Active motifs: inhabited familiarity `ء ه ل:B004/m01`; welcome `ء ه ل:B005/m01`; ease through familiarity `ر س ل:B007/m01`; staying attachment `خ ل د:B002/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:1, 98:6 (أهل); 98:2 (رسول); 98:6, 98:8 (خالدين)
- Synthesis: Habitation supplies the setting, welcome performs admission, and repeated familiarity settles the guest into attachment. The scene is the social inverse of remoteness and wild recoil.

#### Subchannel D. Outsider, Distant Kin, and Aversion
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A newcomer remains socially distant and can be met with the recoil associated with wild creatures.
- Active motifs: stranger and newcomer `ء ت ي:B006/m01`; distant kin `ب ع د:B005/m01`; remote settlement `ك ف ر:B012/m01`; wild aversion from humans `ء ب د:B002/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:1, 98:4, 98:5 (تأتي/أوتوا/يؤتوا); 98:4 (بعد); 98:1, 98:6 (كفروا); 98:8 (أبدا)
- Synthesis: Distance is social before it is spatial: kin become remote, the settlement lies beyond familiar bounds, and the outsider evokes animal-like flight. The cluster gives exclusion a bodily image of recoil.

#### Subchannel E. Servitude and Lower Rank
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A person is placed under ownership, compelled into service, and ranked beneath others.
- Active motifs: slave `ع ب د:B001/m01`; enslavement `ع ب د:B004/m01`; subjugation and ownership `د ي ن:B004/m01`; lower social class `ت ح ت:B002/m01`; distant social rank `ب ع د:B005/m02`
- Ayah anchors: 98:5 (يعبدوا/الدين); 98:8 (تحتها); 98:4 (بعد)
- Synthesis: Ownership defines the relation, enslavement enforces it, and "below" converts hierarchy into spatial rank. This latent social frame sharply contrasts with voluntary worship in the surface-primary devotional channel.

### 9. Partnership, Accord, and Feud
- Semantic invariant: Shared relations are allocated, negotiated, repaired, or broken by hostility.
- Surface relation: direct; 98:4 names dispersal and 98:8 names reciprocal satisfaction, with المشركين providing a surface partnership-form.
- Surprising reach: The field moves from coowned property to consultation and then to feud as a damaged connective bond.

#### Subchannel A. Coownership and Exclusive Allotment
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A resource is shared among partners, divided into portions, and sometimes reserved exclusively for one party.
- Active motifs: coownership `ش ر ك:B001/m01`; exclusive allotment `خ ل ص:B004/m02`; interval between shares `ب ي ن:B002/m01`; paired share `ز ك و:B005/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:1, 98:6 (المشركين); 98:5 (مخلصين/الزكاة); 98:1, 98:4 (البينة)
- Synthesis: Partnership creates the common holding, "between" structures its internal division, pairing establishes matched portions, and exclusive allotment removes one portion from the shared pool.

#### Subchannel B. Consultation and Reconciliation
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Parties deliberate, choose a course, and remove mutual aversion through reciprocal assent.
- Active motifs: counsel `ء م ر:B007/m01`; mutual satisfaction `ر ض و:B003/m01`; reconciliation `ص ل ح:B002/m01`; choosing `خ ي ر:B003/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:5 (أمروا); 98:8 (رضي/رضوا); 98:7 (الصالحات/خير)
- Synthesis: Counsel opens alternatives, selection identifies a course, and mutual satisfaction converts it into accord. Reconciliation names the outcome: the prior state of alienation has been removed.

#### Subchannel C. Bond, Feud, and Peace
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A connective social bond is strained into enmity and can be repaired into peace.
- Active motifs: connective bond `ب ي ن:B003/m01`; close friendship `خ ل ص:B007/m01`; feud `ن و ر:B007/m01`; enmity `ب ع د:B010/m01`; dispute `ش ر ر:B011/m01`; peacemaking `ص ل ح:B002/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:1, 98:4 (البينة); 98:5 (مخلصين); 98:6 (نار/شر); 98:4 (بعد); 98:7 (الصالحات)
- Synthesis: The same "between" that links parties becomes the space in which feud operates. Enmity stretches the relation apart, dispute activates it, and reconciliation restores the bond.

### 10. Unbinding, Separation, and Release
- Semantic invariant: A fastening, obligation, affiliation, or claim is opened so that its parties can part.
- Surface relation: direct; 98:1 uses منفكين and 98:4 uses تفرق and بعد.
- Surprising reach: Physical unfastening extends into manumission, debt release, divorce, banishment, and exoneration.

#### Subchannel A. Opening the Interlock
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Joined parts are opened, a captive or pledge is freed, and the separated pieces continue independently.
- Active motifs: opening an interlock `ف ك ك:B001/m01`; freeing captive or pledge `ف ك ك:B002/m01`; disengaging and continuing `ف ك ك:B003/m01`; separating parts `ف ر ق:B001/m01`; split opening `ف ر ق:B004/m01`; boundary separation `ب ي ن:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:1 (منفكين); 98:4 (تفرق); 98:1, 98:4 (البينة)
- Synthesis: Release begins as mechanics: pressure is removed from an interlock and its parts come apart. Captivity and pledge then inherit the same operation, making freedom a social form of unfastening.

#### Subchannel B. Contractual Manumission
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: An enslaved person enters a written installment agreement that ends ownership and clears the claim.
- Active motifs: emancipation contract `ك ت ب:B005/m01`; enslaved person `ع ب د:B001/m01`; clearing the right `ب ر ء:B004/m01`; escape from constraint `خ ل ص:B002/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:1, 98:3, 98:4, 98:6 (الكتاب/كتب); 98:5 (يعبدوا/مخلصين); 98:6, 98:7 (البرية)
- Synthesis: The written contract transforms release into a staged legal process. Payment or performance clears the owner's right, and the formerly bound person exits the relation.

#### Subchannel C. Dispersal, Banishment, and Desertion
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A group scatters, is driven away, and leaves behind an abandoned relation or place.
- Active motifs: scattering `ف ر ق:B002/m01`; moving apart `ف ر ق:B016/m01`; dispersal in many directions `ع ب د:B010/m01`; active distancing `ب ع د:B003/m01`; banishment or curse `ب ع د:B004/m01`; abandonment `ت ل و:B005/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:4 (تفرق/بعد); 98:5 (يعبدوا); 98:2 (يتلوا)
- Synthesis: Separation becomes a sequence: cohesion fails, members move in divergent directions, and distance hardens into exclusion. Abandonment supplies the state left after the movement is complete.

#### Subchannel D. Irrevocable Marital Parting
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Courtship or marriage is terminated by a separation whose legal effect cannot simply be reversed.
- Active motifs: irrevocable divorce `ب ي ن:B012/m01`; courtship after marriage `ر س ل:B009/m01`; contractual discharge `ب ر ء:B004/m02`; repudiation `ك ف ر:B005/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:1, 98:4 (البينة); 98:2 (رسول); 98:6, 98:7 (البرية); 98:1, 98:6 (كفروا)
- Synthesis: The household bond is first a legal relation and then a terminated one. Repudiation performs the break, discharge clears the obligation, and the divorce branch defines the resulting boundary as final.

#### Subchannel E. Exoneration and Disavowal
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A person separates from blame, a claim, or a condemned affiliation and emerges cleared.
- Active motifs: disassociation and innocence `ب ر ء:B002/m01`; disavowal `ك ف ر:B005/m01`; moral cleansing `ط ه ر:B005/m01`; deliverance `خ ل ص:B002/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:6, 98:7 (البرية); 98:1, 98:6 (كفروا); 98:2 (مطهرة); 98:5 (مخلصين)
- Synthesis: Exoneration is modeled as clean separation. The accused or affiliated subject repudiates the attachment, is purified of its residue, and exits the claim in a state of innocence.

### 11. Abiding, Settlement, and Refuge
- Semantic invariant: A place holds its occupants through permanence, security, familiarity, or concealment.
- Surface relation: direct; 98:8 joins جنات عدن, خالدين, أبدا, عند, and ربهم.
- Surprising reach: The enduring garden is mirrored by hidden shelters, deserted dwellings, and the enclosed place of burial.

#### Subchannel A. Eternal Garden Residence
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: Inhabitants remain in a cultivated abode whose residence and duration do not end.
- Active motifs: eternity `ء ب د:B001/m01`; permanence `خ ل د:B001/m01`; fixed residence `ع د ن:B001/m01`; garden `ج ن ن:B003/m01`; staying `ر ب ب:B007/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:8 (أبدا/خالدين/عدن/جنات/ربهم); 98:6 (خالدين)
- Synthesis: Garden supplies the enclosed living place, عدن and staying make it a settled abode, and the two duration motifs remove temporal limit. The scene is both cultivated space and permanent habitation.

#### Subchannel B. Secure Station and Refuge
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A person reaches a fixed station, enters a concealed shelter, and remains there in safety.
- Active motifs: secure trust `ء م ن:B001/m01`; station `ق و م:B006/m01`; hiding place `ج ن ن:B017/m01`; located place `ك و ن:B002/m01`; lasting stay `ء ب د:B006/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:7 (آمنوا); 98:3, 98:5 (قيمة/يقيموا/القيمة); 98:8 (جنات/أبدا); 98:1 (يكن)
- Synthesis: Station and place supply stability, the hiding place supplies protection, and trust names the resulting condition. Staying converts temporary refuge into residence.

#### Subchannel C. Inhabited and Deserted Places
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Familiar habitation is contrasted with a remote dwelling emptied of its people.
- Active motifs: inhabited familiarity `ء ه ل:B004/m01`; deserted dwelling `ء ب د:B003/m01`; remote settlement `ك ف ر:B012/m01`; spatial distance `ب ع د:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:1, 98:6 (أهل/كفروا); 98:8 (أبدا); 98:4 (بعد)
- Synthesis: Inhabitation is a social quality of place, not merely occupancy. When people withdraw, familiarity becomes desolation and distance turns the abandoned settlement into a remote margin.

#### Subchannel D. Burial and Enclosed Rest
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: The ground is struck or opened, the body is concealed, and the burial place is bounded by earth or wall.
- Active motifs: burial and interment `ج ن ن:B009/m01`; striking the ground `ع د ن:B006/m01`; mountain recess `ك ف ر:B013/m01`; low enclosing wall `ك ف ر:B013/m02`; concealment `ج ن ن:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:8 (جنات/عدن); 98:1, 98:6 (كفروا)
- Synthesis: Ground-striking supplies the action, recess and wall shape the enclosure, and concealment produces the final state. The garden root's enclosing function reaches here into the grave as another held interior.

### 12. Relational Space and Grammar
- Semantic invariant: Meaning is organized by relative position, interval, reference, and temporal or quantitative framing.
- Surface relation: direct; 98:4 uses بعد, 98:8 uses عند and تحت, and 98:5 and 98:8 use ذلك.
- Surprising reach: Concrete spacing becomes pronoun reference, interrogation, softened phonology, and quantification.

#### Subchannel A. Between, Near, Below, and After
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: Entities are placed relative to one another by interval, proximity, vertical order, and posteriority.
- Active motifs: interval between `ب ي ن:B002/m01`; nearness `ع ن د:B004/m01`; below `ت ح ت:B001/m01`; after `ب ع د:B002/m01`; lateral divergence `ع ن د:B002/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:1, 98:4 (البينة); 98:8 (عند/تحتها); 98:4 (بعد)
- Synthesis: The relations form a compact coordinate system. Between and near establish horizontal relation, below establishes vertical order, and after converts the same positioning logic into time.

#### Subchannel B. Gap, Tract, and Expanse
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A wide interval opens into a traversable tract or broad surface.
- Active motifs: wide gap `ب ي ن:B006/m01`; tract or region `ب ي ن:B007/m01`; distance `ب ع د:B001/m01`; emphatic remoteness `ب ع د:B006/m01`; broad plane `ص ح ف:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:1, 98:4 (البينة); 98:4 (بعد); 98:2 (صحفا)
- Synthesis: Separation is spatialized at increasing scale: gap becomes distance, distance becomes tract, and the tract spreads into a broad plane. The channel supplies the landscape through which later road and water scenes move.

#### Subchannel C. Possession, Relative Reference, and Demonstration
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A referent is identified as a possessor, linked by a relative expression, or pointed out demonstratively.
- Active motifs: possession and attribution `ذ و و:B001/m01`; relative connector `ذ و و:B002/m01`; demonstrative pointer `ذ و و:B003/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:5, 98:8 (ذلك)
- Synthesis: Possession anchors a relation to an owner, the relative form carries that relation into a clause, and the demonstrative fixes a referent in discourse. Pointing is therefore a grammatical form of spatial orientation.

#### Subchannel D. Inquiry, Duration, and Quantification
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A speaker asks about an event, locates it within a span, and frames its frequency or amount.
- Active motifs: interrogative compound `ذ و و:B004/m01`; simultaneity or "during" `ب ي ن:B010/m01`; recurring interval `ب ع د:B007/m01`; appointed time `ء م ر:B005/m03`; quantifying preposition `ر ب ب:B015/m01`; distinct kinds and faces `ف ر ق:B015/m01`; intermediate quality `ب ي ن:B011/m01`; softened intermediate phoneme `ب ي ن:B011/m02`
- Ayah anchors: 98:5, 98:8 (ذلك); 98:1, 98:4 (البينة); 98:4 (تفرق/بعد); 98:5 (أمروا); 98:8 (ربهم/ربه)
- Synthesis: Interrogation opens a variable, temporal expressions delimit its span, and quantification constrains recurrence or amount. The "between-between" branch also shows gradation operating inside phonological and qualitative categories.

### 13. Roads, Navigation, and Sequence
- Semantic invariant: Movement is ordered by routes, successive positions, customary courses, and destinations.
- Surface relation: indirect; تأتي, جاءت, يتلوا, and تجري provide surface motion, while the road and gait scenes are lexical extensions.
- Surprising reach: The channel includes road grooves, coastal pilots, second-place horses, and the exhaustion that stops travel.

#### Subchannel A. Road, Fork, and Smoothed Track
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A route is prepared, grooved by passage, divided at a fork, and followed in a chosen direction.
- Active motifs: road and junction `ء ت ي:B010/m01`; road groove `ش ر ك:B005/m01`; traveled road `ع م ل:B011/m01`; road parting `ف ر ق:B006/m01`; smoothed road `ع ب د:B005/m01`; directional inclination `ح ن ف:B002/m01`; reached destination `خ ل ص:B003/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:1, 98:4, 98:5 (تأتي/أوتوا/يؤتوا); 98:1, 98:6 (المشركين); 98:7 (عملوا); 98:4 (تفرق); 98:5 (يعبدوا/حنفاء/مخلصين)
- Synthesis: Repeated passage smooths and grooves the track, the fork divides available movement, and directional inclination selects a branch. The road is thus both material trace and structured choice.

#### Subchannel B. Coastal Navigation
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A maritime chief follows coastal markers through a route exposed to incoming water.
- Active motifs: chief of sailors `ر ب ب:B017/m01`; coast `ع د ن:B003/m01`; road waymark `ء م ر:B005/m02`; beacon `ن و ر:B005/m01`; route junction `ء ت ي:B010/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:8 (ربهم/عدن); 98:5 (أمروا); 98:6 (نار); 98:1, 98:4, 98:5 (تأتي/أوتوا/يؤتوا)
- Synthesis: Captain, coast, waymark, beacon, and route form a complete navigation scene. It joins civic leadership to landscape orientation: command at sea depends on reading fixed signs along a changing edge.

#### Subchannel C. Customary Course
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Repeated conduct settles into a recognized course, habit, or social way.
- Active motifs: customary course `ج ر ي:B002/m01`; custom and established practice `د ي ن:B005/m01`; following remainder `ت ل و:B003/m01`; affair or condition `ء م ر:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:8 (تجري); 98:5 (الدين/دين/أمروا); 98:2 (يتلوا)
- Synthesis: A course first describes movement, then becomes the repeated path of conduct. Remainder and circumstance show how prior action leaves a line that later practice continues to follow.

#### Subchannel D. Procession and Succession
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Members advance in ordered succession, each following the one before it.
- Active motifs: sequential following `ت ل و:B001/m01`; succession `ش ر ك:B007/m01`; successive groups `ر س ل:B005/m01`; recurring intervals `ب ع د:B007/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:2 (يتلوا/رسول); 98:1, 98:6 (المشركين); 98:4 (بعد)
- Synthesis: Following supplies local order, successive groups scale it into a procession, and intervals give the sequence rhythm. Succession preserves continuity while still distinguishing each member.

#### Subchannel E. Gait, Second Position, and Stoppage
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A trained animal moves with an easy gait, takes a ranked position in a race, and can finally tire or stop.
- Active motifs: easy gait `ر س ل:B003/m01`; foreleg gait `ء ت ي:B009/m01`; work-ready mount `ع م ل:B008/m01`; travelers on foot `ع م ل:B012/m01`; second horse `ص ل و:B006/m01`; quick running `ع ب د:B009/m02`; stopping from exhaustion `ق و م:B016/m01`; breakdown of the mount `ع ب د:B011/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:2 (رسول); 98:1, 98:4, 98:5 (تأتي/أوتوا/يؤتوا); 98:7 (عملوا); 98:5 (الصلاة/يعبدوا/يقيموا)
- Synthesis: Aptitude and gait produce sustained movement, the race assigns relative position, and exhaustion terminates the course. The scene captures motion as a progression from readiness through performance to stoppage.

### 14. Hydrology, Coast, and Containment
- Semantic invariant: Water is directed, widened, pooled, or released across a landscape.
- Surface relation: direct; 98:8 presents gardens with rivers flowing beneath them.
- Surprising reach: The river scene extends to floodfronts, shorelines, reservoirs, and broad basins.

#### Subchannel A. Rivers, Canals, and Widening Channels
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Water follows a directed course through a channel that can widen and continue downstream.
- Active motifs: flowing current `ج ر ي:B001/m01`; river `ن ه ر:B001/m01`; watercourse direction `ء ت ي:B004/m01`; channel widening `ن ه ر:B003/m01`; dispatched flow `ر س ل:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:8 (تجري/الأنهار); 98:1, 98:4, 98:5 (تأتي/أوتوا/يؤتوا); 98:2 (رسول)
- Synthesis: Direction determines the course, flow animates it, widening gives the channel capacity, and dispatch supplies release from an upstream source. The surface river is thus both object and continuing process.

#### Subchannel B. Flood, Runoff, and Coast
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Incoming floodwater runs along a side channel and reaches a coastal edge.
- Active motifs: incoming flood `ء ت ي:B005/m01`; heavy rain from a cloud `ع ن د:B003/m02`; coast `ع د ن:B003/m01`; dark vastness `ك ف ر:B002/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:1, 98:4, 98:5 (تأتي/أوتوا/يؤتوا); 98:8 (عند/عدن); 98:1, 98:6 (كفروا)
- Synthesis: Flood supplies force, side-flow supplies direction, coast supplies the receiving boundary, and dark vastness supplies scale. Together they form a shoreline scene under pressure from arriving water.

#### Subchannel C. Pool, Basin, and Reservoir
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Water collects in a depression or broad vessel and is held as a usable reserve.
- Active motifs: pool `ج ي ء:B002/m01`; small water basin `ص ح ف:B004/m02`; abundant stored water `ر ب ب:B013/m01`; low enclosing wall `ك ف ر:B013/m02`
- Ayah anchors: 98:4 (جاءت); 98:2 (صحفا); 98:8 (ربهم/ربه); 98:1, 98:6 (كفروا)
- Synthesis: Depression receives the water, breadth gives the basin capacity, and abundance turns collection into reserve. The low wall supplies a practical boundary that keeps the stored water in place.

### 15. Growth, Fertility, and Sustenance
- Semantic invariant: Hidden potential becomes plant, offspring, milk, or harvest through increase and release.
- Surface relation: direct; 98:5 names الزكاة and 98:8 names gardens and flowing rivers.
- Surprising reach: Growth reaches seed-covering farmers, lactating ewes, wild herds, husks, and sun-dried fruit.

#### Subchannel A. Seed, Increase, and Yield
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A farmer covers seed, growth increases under protection, and the crop gives a yield.
- Active motifs: farmer covering seed `ك ف ر:B008/m01`; growth `ز ك و:B001/m01`; agricultural yield `ء ت ي:B007/m01`; increase and blessing `ء م ر:B004/m01`; protective husk `ك ف ر:B010/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:1, 98:6 (كفروا); 98:5 (الزكاة/أمروا); 98:1, 98:4, 98:5 (تأتي/أوتوا/يؤتوا)
- Synthesis: Covering is productive here: seed is hidden so that increase can occur. The husk preserves development, and yield is the moment when concealed growth becomes available.

#### Subchannel B. Garden, Thicket, Pasture, and Blossom
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Dense vegetation forms a garden whose plants provide pasture and visible blossom.
- Active motifs: thick vegetation `ج ن ن:B011/m01`; evergreen plant `ر ب ب:B012/m01`; pasture plant `ص ل و:B009/m01`; blossom `ن و ر:B004/m01`; named plant `ش ر ر:B012/m01`; scattered vegetation `ف ر ق:B014/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:8 (جنات/ربهم); 98:5 (الصلاة); 98:6 (نار/شر); 98:4 (تفرق)
- Synthesis: Vegetation moves from scattered plants to thicket and garden, while pasture gives it use and blossom gives it seasonal visibility. Density and dispersion are complementary phases of the same ecology.

#### Subchannel C. Lactation, Fertile Animals, and Herd
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A recently delivered animal remains near shelter, gives milk, and rejoins a managed herd.
- Active motifs: milk flow `ر س ل:B006/m01`; newly delivered ewe `ر ب ب:B009/m01`; annually fertile female `ء ب د:B004/m01`; wild cattle herd `ر ب ب:B014/m01`; pregnant animal leaving the herd `ف ر ق:B008/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:2 (رسول); 98:8 (ربهم/أبدا); 98:4 (تفرق)
- Synthesis: Parturition changes herd position: the pregnant animal separates, the newly delivered ewe remains close for milk, and fertility returns the animal to recurring production. Herd and milk connect reproduction to sustenance.

#### Subchannel D. Ripening, Drying, and Withering
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Produce matures, dries under exposure, and can end as husk or shriveled fruit.
- Active motifs: shriveled date `خ ش ي:B004/m01`; sun-drying `ش ر ر:B002/m01`; husk `ك ف ر:B010/m01`; yield before drying `ء ت ي:B007/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:8 (خشي); 98:6 (شر); 98:1, 98:6 (كفروا); 98:1, 98:4, 98:5 (تأتي/أوتوا/يؤتوا)
- Synthesis: Yield marks fullness, exposure removes moisture, and husk or shriveled fruit marks the terminal state. The sequence can describe preservation or failed fertility depending on what remains edible.

### 16. Covering, Light, and Sky-Time
- Semantic invariant: Visibility changes as things are covered, illuminated, exposed by day, or massed into cloud.
- Surface relation: direct; 98:6 names fire and 98:8 names rivers and permanence within a garden landscape.
- Surprising reach: The field reaches night, meridian shadow, month endings, layered cloud, and sparks.

#### Subchannel A. Concealment, Night, and Dark Vastness
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Covering thickens into night and makes a broad region difficult to see.
- Active motifs: concealment `ج ن ن:B001/m01`; night `ج ن ن:B002/m01`; covering `ك ف ر:B001/m01`; dark vastness `ك ف ر:B002/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:8 (جنات); 98:1, 98:6 (كفروا)
- Synthesis: Covering is the operation, concealment the immediate state, night the temporal setting, and dark vastness the enlarged landscape. The same lexical motion hides objects and whole horizons.

#### Subchannel B. Fire, Illumination, and Sparks
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Heat kindles visible fire, throws sparks, and becomes a source of illumination or warning.
- Active motifs: illumination `ن و ر:B001/m01`; fire `ن و ر:B002/m01`; sparks `ش ر ر:B003/m01`; heat and burning `ص ل و:B001/m01`; beacon `ن و ر:B005/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:6 (نار/شر); 98:5 (الصلاة)
- Synthesis: Burning and heat generate sparks, while light converts combustion into visibility. The beacon gives the fire a directional function: it warns or guides beyond its immediate heat.

#### Subchannel C. Daylight, Meridian, and Calendar Edge
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Day advances toward noon while a calendar unit approaches its final night or day.
- Active motifs: daylight `ن ه ر:B002/m01`; noon and meridian `ق و م:B017/m01`; final night or day of a month `ب ر ء:B006/m01`; appointed time `ء م ر:B005/m03`
- Ayah anchors: 98:8 (الأنهار); 98:3, 98:5 (قيمة/يقيموا/القيمة); 98:6, 98:7 (البرية); 98:5 (أمروا)
- Synthesis: Noon locates the day spatially through the sun's height, while the month's last day locates a larger cycle at its boundary. Appointed time unifies both scales as measured arrival.

#### Subchannel D. Layered Cloud and Overcast
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Moisture gathers into layered cloud, spreads a covering overhead, and releases side-running rain.
- Active motifs: layered cloud `ر ب ب:B008/m01`; cloud mass `ن ه ر:B008/m01`; covering canopy `ك ف ر:B001/m01`; heavy rain `ع ن د:B003/m02`
- Ayah anchors: 98:8 (ربهم/الأنهار/عند); 98:1, 98:6 (كفروا)
- Synthesis: Cloud is built as a massed covering rather than an isolated object. Layering darkens the sky, and released rain converts the overhead accumulation into lateral flow on the ground.

### 17. Reproduction, Maturation, and Bodily Cycles
- Semantic invariant: Bodies pass through conception, birth, fertility, clearance, youth, and age.
- Surface relation: indirect; the cited roots occur throughout 98:1-8, while their reproductive senses are latent lexical branches.
- Surprising reach: The channel includes fetal concealment, estrus, postpartum clearance, youthful zeal, and an elder defined by saying "I used to."

#### Subchannel A. Fetus, Birth, and Offspring
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A fetus remains concealed, birth opens the body, and offspring emerge under the pressure of the pelvic flank.
- Active motifs: fetus `ج ن ن:B007/m01`; offspring `ت ل و:B006/m01`; pelvic flank at birth `ص ل و:B005/m01`; parturition `ف ك ك:B007/m01`; newborn lamb `ء م ر:B009/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:8 (جنات); 98:2 (يتلوا); 98:5 (الصلاة/أمروا); 98:1 (منفكين)
- Synthesis: Concealment supplies gestation, opening supplies delivery, and the pelvic motif supplies bodily mechanics. Offspring and lamb name the resulting new life.

#### Subchannel B. Estrus, Pairing, and Herd Separation
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: An animal enters estrus, pairs, and temporarily separates from the herd during pregnancy.
- Active motifs: estrus `ء ت ي:B012/m01`; estrus and reproductive opening `ف ك ك:B007/m02`; paired state `ز ك و:B005/m01`; pregnant animal leaving the herd `ف ر ق:B008/m01`; newly delivered ewe `ر ب ب:B009/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:1, 98:4, 98:5 (تأتي/أوتوا/يؤتوا); 98:1 (منفكين); 98:5 (الزكاة); 98:4 (تفرق); 98:8 (ربهم)
- Synthesis: Pairing initiates the cycle, pregnancy changes the animal's social position, and delivery returns the scene to fertility and milk. Separation here is a temporary reproductive behavior rather than social rupture.

#### Subchannel C. Menstrual and Postpartum Clearance
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Bleeding ceases, washing follows, and an adolescent or postpartum woman returns to a cleared state.
- Active motifs: menstrual clearance `ب ر ء:B005/m01`; cessation of menstruation `ط ه ر:B002/m01`; washing `ط ه ر:B003/m01`; maiden `ج ر ي:B004/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:6, 98:7 (البرية); 98:2 (مطهرة); 98:8 (تجري)
- Synthesis: Cessation marks the biological boundary, washing performs the hygienic transition, and clearance names the restored state. The maiden motif places the process within a broader female life cycle.

#### Subchannel D. Youth, Freshness, and Old Age
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Life begins in fresh youth, intensifies into youthful zeal, and later becomes retrospective old age.
- Active motifs: beginning of youth `ج ن ن:B014/m01`; youthful freshness `ر ب ب:B009/m02`; youthful zeal `ش ر ر:B010/m01`; period of girlhood `ج ر ي:B004/m02`; aged man saying "I used to" `ك و ن:B005/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:8 (جنات/ربهم/تجري); 98:6 (شر); 98:1 (يكن)
- Synthesis: Fresh beginning develops into energy and then into remembered past. The elder's repeated "I used to" makes age a grammatical habit of looking back at former vitality.

### 18. Anatomy, Injury, and Recovery
- Semantic invariant: Bodily parts bear motion and pain, become injured or dislocated, and receive treatment that restores function.
- Surface relation: indirect; roots such as فكك, فرق, عمل, برء, and طهر are present, while the anatomical and medical senses are lexical.
- Surprising reach: The field reaches jaws, ribs, blind-looking eyes, suppurating wounds, oral dosing, and postpartum remedies.

#### Subchannel A. Frame, Limbs, and Joints
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A body is assembled from jaws, chest bones, limbs, joints, and a curved foot.
- Active motifs: jaws `ف ك ك:B004/m01`; chest bones and rib ends `ج ن ن:B016/m01`; working legs `ع م ل:B010/m01`; working eye `ع م ل:B010/m02`; anatomical separation `ف ر ق:B007/m01`; limb separation `ب ي ن:B008/m01`; curved foot `ح ن ف:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:1 (منفكين); 98:8 (جنات); 98:7 (عملوا); 98:4 (تفرق); 98:1, 98:4 (البينة); 98:5 (حنفاء)
- Synthesis: The branches map a load-bearing frame whose parts meet at separable joints. Curvature and separation make bodily alignment visible by showing where the frame can deviate or come apart.

#### Subchannel B. Pain, Disease, and Wound Discharge
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A limb or organ becomes painful, gait fails, and an injured site releases pus.
- Active motifs: wound pus `ج ي ء:B003/m01`; wound discharge `ج ي ء:B006/m01`; side-flowing blood or vomit `ع ن د:B003/m01`; pain lodged in an organ `ق و م:B019/m01`; leg disease in a sheep `ق و م:B020/m01`; dislocation `ف ك ك:B005/m01`; gaunt weakness `ف ك ك:B007/m03`; calamity or death `ء ت ي:B011/m01`; last breath `ت ل و:B008/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:4 (جاءت); 98:8 (عند); 98:3, 98:5 (قيمة/يقيموا/القيمة); 98:1 (منفكين); 98:1, 98:4, 98:5 (تأتي/أوتوا/يؤتوا); 98:2 (يتلوا)
- Synthesis: Pain first settles in a part, then alters stance or gait, while dislocation and suppuration reveal structural injury. Calamity and last breath mark the extreme end of the same bodily decline.

#### Subchannel C. Convalescence and Release from Illness
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Disease loosens, the patient separates from the afflicted state, and ordinary function returns.
- Active motifs: recovery `ب ر ء:B003/m01`; convalescence `ف ر ق:B010/m01`; escape from constraint `خ ل ص:B002/m01`; cleansing wash `ط ه ر:B003/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:6, 98:7 (البرية); 98:4 (تفرق); 98:5 (مخلصين); 98:2 (مطهرة)
- Synthesis: Recovery is expressed as separation: illness releases its hold and the patient exits it. Washing removes residue, while convalescence names the interval in which restored function becomes stable.

#### Subchannel D. Dosing and Medicinal Preparation
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A remedy is prepared from food or aromatic material, purified, and placed into a child's mouth.
- Active motifs: oral dosing of a child `ف ك ك:B009/m01`; date-and-fenugreek remedy `ف ر ق:B012/m01`; dried medicinal meat `خ ش ي:B004/m02`; purifying medium `ط ه ر:B004/m01`; camphor `ك ف ر:B011/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:1 (منفكين); 98:4 (تفرق); 98:8 (خشي); 98:2 (مطهرة); 98:1, 98:6 (كفروا)
- Synthesis: Preparation combines drying, cooking, and purification before the medicine is administered orally. The postpartum remedy and camphor broaden treatment from dosing into restorative food and aromatic material.

### 19. Craft, Materials, and Fitted Objects
- Semantic invariant: Separate materials are cut, joined, fitted, ornamented, or processed into usable objects.
- Surface relation: indirect; الكتاب, عملوا, مطهرة, and نار provide the active roots, while the craft scenes are latent.
- Surprising reach: The field reconstructs spear assembly, patched vessels, jewelry, rendered fat, perfume grinding, and body grooming.

#### Subchannel A. Spear and Projectile Assembly
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A shaft is fitted to its forward metal component and prepared as a projectile.
- Active motifs: spearhead fitting `ء م ر:B011/m01`; spear shaft and forepart `ع م ل:B009/m01`; short arrow `ر س ل:B011/m01`; chest-bone forepart analogy `ج ن ن:B016/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:5 (أمروا); 98:7 (عملوا); 98:2 (رسول); 98:8 (جنات)
- Synthesis: Shaft and fitted point provide the actual assembly, while short arrow and chest forepart extend it by scale and shape. The shared invariant is a rigid forward member designed to project force.

#### Subchannel B. Stitching, Strap, Patch, and Knot
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Flexible material is stitched, strapped, patched at a weak edge, and secured by a tight knot.
- Active motifs: stitching and joining `ك ت ب:B001/m01`; cutting and shredding `ش ر ر:B004/m01`; sandal strap `ش ر ك:B004/m01`; vessel-bottom patch `ع د ن:B004/m01`; shoe extension patch `ع د ن:B004/m02`; tar-coated vessel `ع ب د:B005/m02`; tight knot `ر ب ب:B016/m02`; fringe `ش ر ر:B006/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:1, 98:3, 98:4, 98:6 (الكتاب/كتب); 98:1, 98:6 (المشركين); 98:8 (عدن/ربهم); 98:5 (يعبدوا); 98:6 (شر)
- Synthesis: Joining closes a seam, strap and knot hold tension, and a patch adds material where a vessel or shoe needs reinforcement. Fringes show the loose edge that this craft work controls.

#### Subchannel C. Jewelry, Necklace, and Crown
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Small joined pieces become wearable ornaments and, at the highest rank, a crown.
- Active motifs: earrings and bracelets `خ ل د:B003/m01`; necklace `ر س ل:B011/m02`; crown `ك ف ر:B015/m01`; joining `ك ت ب:B001/m01`; craftspeople `ع م ل:B006/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:6, 98:8 (خالدين); 98:2 (رسول); 98:1, 98:6 (كفروا); 98:1, 98:3, 98:4, 98:6 (الكتاب/كتب); 98:7 (عملوا)
- Synthesis: Joining converts discrete pieces into ornaments that encircle the body. Crown extends the same fitted form into public rank, while the worker motif supplies the maker.

#### Subchannel D. Fat, Ghee, Syrup, and Concentrate
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Animal or food material is rendered, clarified, and concentrated into fat, ghee, or syrup.
- Active motifs: rendered fat or oil `ء ه ل:B006/m01`; kidney fat `ف ر ق:B013/m01`; clarified butter `خ ل ص:B008/m01`; food residue `خ ل ص:B008/m02`; thick syrup `ر ب ب:B006/m01`; dripping roast fat `ش ر ر:B005/m01`; fragments after biting `ش ر ر:B004/m02`
- Ayah anchors: 98:1, 98:6 (أهل); 98:4 (تفرق); 98:5 (مخلصين); 98:8 (ربهم); 98:6 (شر)
- Synthesis: Heat releases fat, clarification separates usable concentrate from residue, and continued reduction produces syrup-like thickness. The scene is a sequence of material transformation rather than a loose food list.

#### Subchannel E. Perfume, Kohl, Lime, and Grinding
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Aromatic or cosmetic material is ground on a slab, smoked or mixed into pigment, and applied to skin or hair.
- Active motifs: perfume slab `ع ب د:B012/m01`; grinding stone `ص ل و:B008/m01`; kohl smoke `ن و ر:B008/m01`; tattoo pigment `ن و ر:B008/m02`; depilatory lime `ن و ر:B009/m01`; mustache fullness `ع د ن:B007/m01`; purifying medium `ط ه ر:B004/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:5 (يعبدوا/الصلاة); 98:6 (نار); 98:8 (عدن); 98:2 (مطهرة)
- Synthesis: Grinding prepares the aromatic base, smoke or mineral supplies pigment, and lime alters body hair. Mustache fullness gives the grooming scene its bodily target, while purification governs preparation and application.

### 20. Strategy, Capture, and Contest
- Semantic invariant: Agents seek advantage by hiding, trapping, shielding, competing, or coercing.
- Surface relation: indirect; separation, disbelief, and fire supply surface tension, while the tactical scenes are lexical.
- Surprising reach: The channel includes hunter blinds, burrowing prey, shields, wrestling, and forced disobedience.

#### Subchannel A. Ambush, Snare, and Lure
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A hunter hides in a blind, lures prey from shelter, and catches it in a snare or trap.
- Active motifs: snare `ش ر ك:B006/m01`; trap `ص ل و:B004/m01`; hunter's blind `ب ر ء:B007/m01`; concealed refuge `ج ن ن:B017/m01`; luring an animal from its burrow `خ ي ر:B006/m01`; sudden snatch `ن ه ر:B006/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:1, 98:6 (المشركين); 98:5 (الصلاة); 98:6, 98:7 (البرية); 98:8 (جنات/خير/الأنهار)
- Synthesis: Concealment protects the hunter, lure initiates movement, and trap converts that movement into capture. The snatch is the abrupt terminal action of the same tactical sequence.

#### Subchannel B. Small Prey, Serpent, and Blindness
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Small harmful creatures inhabit concealed ground and are pursued or detected despite impaired sight.
- Active motifs: serpent `ج ن ن:B012/m01`; blind burrowing rodent `خ ل د:B005/m01`; gnat-like irritant `ش ر ر:B009/m01`; lure from a burrow `خ ي ر:B006/m01`; intact-looking blind eye `ق و م:B021/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:8 (جنات/خالدين); 98:6 (شر); 98:7 (خير); 98:3, 98:5 (قيمة/يقيموا/القيمة)
- Synthesis: Burrow and concealment define the habitat, serpent and irritant define danger, and blindness complicates pursuit or perception. The intact eye with lost sight gives a bodily analogue to the unseen movement underground.

#### Subchannel C. Shielding and Structural Cover
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A rigid cover receives force and is held by a standing support.
- Active motifs: shield `ج ن ن:B008/m01`; covering `ك ف ر:B001/m01`; standing support `ق و م:B012/m02`; strength and solidity `ع ب د:B007/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:8 (جنات); 98:1, 98:6 (كفروا); 98:3, 98:5 (قيمة/يقيموا/القيمة); 98:5 (يعبدوا)
- Synthesis: Covering becomes defensive when it is rigid, supported, and strong enough to receive impact. The scene turns concealment into active protection.

#### Subchannel D. Rivalry, Wrestling, and Overcoming
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Matched opponents engage, resist, and seek to overcome one another.
- Active motifs: overcoming in reciprocal action `ج ز ي:B005/m01`; resistance and wrestling `ق و م:B014/m01`; matching competition `ج ر ي:B007/m01`; victory in a satisfaction contest `ر ض و:B005/m01`; victory in contest `ر ض ي:B002/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:8 (جزاؤهم/تجري/رضي/رضوا); 98:3, 98:5 (قيمة/يقيموا/القيمة)
- Synthesis: Matching creates comparable opponents, resistance sustains the encounter, and overcoming produces an asymmetric result. The satisfaction branches preserve the interpersonal setting by describing victory over another claimant.

#### Subchannel E. Coercion and Rebellion
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Pressure forces a subject toward disobedience, and the subject may harden into opposition.
- Active motifs: compulsion `ج ي ء:B005/m01`; coercion into disobedience `ك ف ر:B007/m01`; obstinate opposition `ع ن د:B001/m01`; enmity `ب ع د:B010/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 98:4 (جاءت/بعد); 98:1, 98:6 (كفروا); 98:8 (عند)
- Synthesis: Compulsion applies force, coerced disobedience redirects conduct, and obstinacy makes resistance durable. Enmity is the social state that remains after pressure and rebellion polarize the parties.

### 21. Decision, Deliberation, and Extremity
- Semantic invariant: A course is weighed, chosen, enacted decisively, weakened by indecision, or driven to an emotional limit.
