- mechanism: The verse first defines a rejecting response, then locates its bearers within communities of textual belonging and association. The verdict therefore turns on enacted rejection or concealment, not on a bare communal name by itself.
- trace:
  - 98:6 **كَفَرُوا۟** ك ف ر B003: Covering or rejecting truth supplies the conduct that grammatically scopes the condemned class.
  - 98:6 **أَهْلِ** ء ه ل B001: A people bound by closeness and belonging supplies the communal affiliation from which the rejecting class is named.
  - 98:6 **ٱلْكِتَٰبِ** ك ت ب B002: Joined letters and the written book identify the textual object around which one named community belongs.
  - 98:6 **وَٱلْمُشْرِكِينَ** ش ر ك B002: Association of a partner with God names the second response-form placed under the verdict.

## concealment exposure
- reading: The fire also enacts a reversal from concealed truth to burning exposure and visible marking.
- mechanism: The branch images create a material reversal: truth is covered, but the resulting fire burns, brands, and stands visibly like a beacon. Punishment is thus readable not only as heat but as exposure of what concealment tried to suppress.
- trace:
  - 98:6 **كَفَرُوا۟** ك ف ر B003: Concealment or rejection of truth supplies the initially hidden object in the reversal.
  - 98:6 **نَارِ** ن و ر B002: Burning fire and branding supply both the literal punishment and a public mark.
  - 98:6 **نَارِ** ن و ر B005: The visible beacon or landmark image turns the fire into an exploratory mechanism of disclosure.

## enduring attachment and rank
- reading: It binds enduring habitation to a comparative status within creation, presenting outcome and evaluation as one stable condition.
- mechanism: خالدين contributes both resistant duration and clinging residence; شر supplies badness, and البرية places that valuation within created beings. The sentence presents a durable condition of attachment and rank, not a momentary punitive event.
- trace:
  - 98:6 **خَٰلِدِينَ** خ ل د B001: Enduring permanence supplies the temporal stability of residence in the fire.
  - 98:6 **خَٰلِدِينَ** خ ل د B002: Clinging and staying close supply the spatial-affective image of attachment to the stated condition.
  - 98:6 **شَرُّ** ش ر ر B001: Evil and badness supply the negative pole of the final comparative classification.
  - 98:6 **ٱلْبَرِيَّةِ** ب ر ء B001: Creation and bringing into being define the field within which the negative rank is assigned.

## release at disclosure
- reading: The category is read against a release threshold: clear evidence arrives, and the focus judges the rejection that remains operative in its wake.
- mechanism: The repeated rejecting subjects of 98:1 are described through non-release until the clear evidence arrives. Release from an entanglement and disclosure of what was hidden turn 98:6 from a static identity verdict into the endpoint of a tested response to clarification.
- trace:
  - 98:6 **كَفَرُوا۟** ك ف ر B003: Rejection of truth anchors the revised reading in the focus predicate.
  - 98:1 **كَفَرُوا۟** ك ف ر B003: The same rejection image identifies the antecedent group whose later verdict appears in the focus.
  - 98:1 **مُنفَكِّينَ** ف ك ك B001: Opening what is locked and separating what is entangled supplies the release that the subjects await.
  - 98:1 **ٱلْبَيِّنَةُ** ب ي ن B004: Manifestation and disclosure supply the threshold at which continued concealment becomes answerable.

## scripture as live measure
- reading: Their claimed belonging is tested by how they receive, follow, and align with the newly delivered purified writing.
- mechanism: The book of communal belonging is re-presented as a messenger's recitation of purified pages containing upright writings. Static possession is displaced by a live sequence of reception, following, purification, and alignment, making true textual belonging a tested relation rather than an inherited shield.
- trace:
  - 98:6 **أَهْلِ** ء ه ل B003: Fitness or worthiness makes belonging to the book available as a qualitative relation, not merely a label.
  - 98:6 **ٱلْكِتَٰبِ** ك ت ب B002: The written book supplies the focus identity's textual anchor.
  - 98:2 **رَسُولٌ** ر س ل B002: Messenger and message turn the evidence into a delivered communicative event.
  - 98:2 **يَتْلُوا۟** ت ل و B001: Following and succession underlie the literal recitation and activate a possible demand to follow what is recited.
  - 98:2 **صُحُفًا** ص ح ف B002: Written pages give the delivered evidence a concrete textual vehicle.
  - 98:2 **مُّطَهَّرَةً** ط ه ر B005: Removal of moral ugliness supplies the pages' purifying normative direction.
  - 98:3 **قَيِّمَةٌ** ق و م B008: Uprightness, balance, and straightness characterize the writings as a measure of alignment.

## post clarity faction
- reading: It names a rejecting faction whose decisive shape emerges through rupture after received text and arrived clarity.
- mechanism: 98:4 opposes the book's joining function to the bearers' post-disclosure splitting. Being given the book precedes rather than prevents factional rupture, so the focus class is readable as a faction crystallized by response to clarity rather than as a primordial communal essence.
- trace:
  - 98:6 **ٱلْكِتَٰبِ** ك ت ب B001: Joining one thing to another supplies the latent integrating function of the focus's book.
  - 98:4 **أُوتُوا۟** ء ت ي B002: Giving supplies the prior endowment in those who were given the book.
  - 98:4 **ٱلْكِتَٰبَ** ك ت ب B002: The written book identifies what the later-dividing community had already received.
  - 98:4 **تَفَرَّقَ** ف ر ق B002: Scattering into parts supplies the social result that reverses the book's joining image.
  - 98:4 **ٱلْبَيِّنَةُ** ب ي ن B004: Disclosure supplies the event after which the division occurs.

## failed purification and alignment
- reading: They are also failures of a commanded process that purifies allegiance, reorients conduct, and establishes socially enacted worship.
- mechanism: 98:5 forms a corrective process: remove mixture, become exclusive from partnership, turn toward one direction, establish an upright practice, and purify through giving. Against it, كفر and شرك in the focus become a functional failure of clarification, exclusive alignment, and enacted repair rather than only abstract doctrinal labels.
- trace:
  - 98:6 **كَفَرُوا۟** ك ف ر B003: Covering or rejecting truth anchors the failure of clarification in the focus.
  - 98:6 **وَٱلْمُشْرِكِينَ** ش ر ك B001: Sharing and partnership supply the mixed allegiance that the context's exclusivity reverses.
  - 98:5 **مُخْلِصِينَ** خ ل ص B001: Purification and clarity after mixture supply the context's primary corrective operation.
  - 98:5 **مُخْلِصِينَ** خ ل ص B004: Exclusive separation from partnership directly reverses the focus's sharing relation.
  - 98:5 **حُنَفَآءَ** ح ن ف B002: Inclining toward a direction supplies active reorientation rather than static affiliation.
  - 98:5 **وَيُقِيمُوا۟ ٱلْقَيِّمَةِ** ق و م B008: Straightness and balance supply the established form of the commanded practice and religion.
  - 98:5 **ٱلزَّكَوٰةَ** ز ك و B002: Purity and soundness make giving part of the same enacted repair.

## mirrored criterion pair
- reading: It is the negative pole of a mirrored classification whose contrasting criteria are settled belief and fitting action.
- mechanism: 98:7 repeats the focus's إن الذين ... أولئك هم ... البرية frame while reversing both predicate and valuation. Belief joined to intentional, fitting action supplies the positive pole, making شر البرية in the focus a criterion-linked outcome within a deliberate pair rather than an isolated essentializing label.
- trace:
  - 98:6 **شَرُّ** ش ر ر B001: Evil and badness supply the negative evaluative pole in the focus.
  - 98:6 **ٱلْبَرِيَّةِ** ب ر ء B001: Created beings supply the shared comparison class held constant across the pair.
  - 98:7 **ءَامَنُوا۟** ء م ن B002: Heart-settling assent supplies the positive response opposed to rejection.
  - 98:7 **وَعَمِلُوا۟** ع م ل B001: Intentional action prevents the positive classification from resting on profession alone.
  - 98:7 **ٱلصَّٰلِحَٰتِ** ص ل ح B003: Fitness for a thing characterizes the deeds as functionally appropriate and repairing.
  - 98:7 **خَيْرُ** خ ي ر B002: Excellence and chosen goodness supply the positive valuation that directly mirrors شر.
  - 98:7 **ٱلْبَرِيَّةِ** ب ر ء B001: The repeated creation image confirms that both evaluations operate over the same field.

## asymmetric duration marking
- reading: 98:6 itself asserts enduring abiding, but the context reserves an extra explicit perpetuity marker for 98:8; no conclusion about finite punishment follows from that asymmetry alone.
- mechanism: Both outcomes use خالدين فيها, but only 98:8 adds أبدا. The shared خلد branch supports durable abiding in 98:6, while the context's additional explicit duration marker cautions against silently importing every feature of the positive clause into the focus.
- trace:
  - 98:6 **خَٰلِدِينَ** خ ل د B001: Enduring permanence supplies the focus's real but unaugmented duration claim.
  - 98:8 **خَٰلِدِينَ** خ ل د B001: The repeated enduring-permanence image establishes the exact lexical symmetry between the two outcomes.
  - 98:8 **أَبَدًا** ء ب د B001: Long duration and perpetuity supply an explicit intensifier present only on the positive side.

## negative recompense equivalence
- reading: Alongside its ordinary negative force, it faintly reads as a matched adverse valuation in which chosen response and resulting condition answer one another.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 98:6 **شَرُّ** ش ر ر B002: The non-dominant mapped branch of equivalence and matching recasts the negative rank as an exploratory matched valuation.
  - 98:7 **وَعَمِلُوا۟** ع م ل B001: Intentional action supplies the conduct to which a valuation or outcome can correspond.
  - 98:8 **جَزَآؤُهُمْ** ج ز ي B001: Matching an act with its recompense supplies the context-side reciprocity mechanism.

## two covering habitats
- reading: The sequence supports an exploratory bifurcation: covering truth belongs to an enclosing fire-world, whereas arboreal covering constitutes a garden-world; relation and function determine the image's polarity.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 98:6 **كَفَرُوا۟** ك ف ر B001: Concrete covering supplies the neutral material operation whose moral direction is tested.
  - 98:6 **كَفَرُوا۟** ك ف ر B003: Covering truth specifies the destructive use of that operation in the focus.
  - 98:6 **نَارِ** ن و ر B002: Burning fire supplies the enclosing punitive habitat that follows concealment.
  - 98:8 **جَنَّٰتُ** ج ن ن B003: A garden covered by trees supplies the life-giving contextual counterpart to destructive covering.


# Focus 98:7

## embodied assent
- reading: Those whose settled assent becomes deliberate anti-corruptive action are marked as the choice among created beings.
- mechanism: Settled assent becomes intentional action that opposes corruption; khayr then evaluates this integrated disposition as chosen excellence within the field of created beings.
- trace:
  - 98:7 **ءَامَنُوا۟** ء م ن B002: Trusting assent supplies inward confirmation rather than a bare group label.
  - 98:7 **وَعَمِلُوا۟** ع م ل B001: Intentional doing turns assent into purposive enactment.
  - 98:7 **ٱلصَّٰلِحَٰتِ** ص ل ح B001: Soundness opposed to corruption specifies the action's corrective quality.
  - 98:7 **خَيْرُ** خ ي ر B002: Virtue and chosen excellence supply the evaluative force of the predicate.
  - 98:7 **ٱلْبَرِيَّةِ** ب ر ء B001: Creation fixes the comparison class as created beings.

## relational repair
- reading: The verse commends trust made socially operative through dealings that remove hostility and produce benefit among created beings.
- mechanism: Security and trust issue in dealings that remove estrangement between people; excellence is therefore measured by beneficial repair within created social life.
- trace:
  - 98:7 **ءَامَنُوا۟** ء م ن B001: Settled security and trust provide the relational condition from which repair can proceed.
  - 98:7 **وَعَمِلُوا۟** ع م ل B005: Dealings between people locate action in concrete social exchange.
  - 98:7 **ٱلصَّٰلِحَٰتِ** ص ل ح B002: Reconciliation and removal of hostility make those dealings reparative.
  - 98:7 **خَيْرُ** خ ي ر B001: Desired beneficial good makes social benefit the axis of excellence.
  - 98:7 **ٱلْبَرِيَّةِ** ب ر ء B001: Created beings supply the community in which the repair has effect.

## fitting use
- reading: The superior created response is to put capacities to fitting, beneficial use.
- mechanism: Putting capacities to work and fitting action to its object produce benefit; excellence can thus be heard functionally as the right use of created capacities.
- trace:
  - 98:7 **وَعَمِلُوا۟** ع م ل B002: Putting something to work supplies an operational rather than merely classificatory sense of action.
  - 98:7 **ٱلصَّٰلِحَٰتِ** ص ل ح B003: Suitability and fitness govern whether the applied capacity matches its object.
  - 98:7 **خَيْرُ** خ ي ر B001: Beneficial good supplies the outcome by which fitting use is valued.
  - 98:7 **ٱلْبَرِيَّةِ** ب ر ء B001: Creation supplies the larger order whose capacities are being used fittingly.

## disclosure tests enactment
- reading: Clarity can precede fragmentation, so faith here is the settled, enacted reception of disclosure that bears cohesive and reparative fruit.
- mechanism: The sequence moves from disclosed meaning through purified pages and upright contents to fragmentation after disclosure. This revises faith from mere exposure to evidence into a settled response demonstrated by reparative action.
- trace:
  - 98:7 **ءَامَنُوا۟** ء م ن B002: Inwardly settled confirmation supplies the receptive response whose quality is being tested.
  - 98:7 **وَعَمِلُوا۟** ع م ل B001: Intentional action makes reception observable as conduct.
  - 98:7 **ٱلصَّٰلِحَٰتِ** ص ل ح B002: Reconciliation supplies the cohesive alternative to post-disclosure division.
  - 98:1 **ٱلْبَيِّنَةُ** ب ي ن B005: Meaning disclosed by speech or sign supplies the initial evidence event.
  - 98:2 **مُّطَهَّرَةً** ط ه ر B001: Purity and removal of stain intensify the clean presentation of the evidence.
  - 98:3 **قَيِّمَةٌ** ق و م B008: Uprightness and evenness characterize the disclosed writings as normatively straight.
  - 98:4 **ٱلْبَيِّنَةُ** ب ي ن B005: Repeated disclosed meaning marks the same clarity as preceding the divergent response.
  - 98:4 **تَفَرَّقَ** ف ر ق B002: Scattering into parts supplies the outcome that clear presentation alone does not prevent.

## commanded practice system
- reading: Righteous deeds are concretized as an undivided and maintained praxis joining worship to growth-bearing social transfer.
- mechanism: Obligation, exclusive devotion, directional orientation, maintained worship, giving, and growth unfold al-salihat as a coordinated practice. It orders the agent while circulating benefit outward.
- trace:
  - 98:7 **وَعَمِلُوا۟** ع م ل B001: Intentional doing receives concrete content from the preceding command sequence.
  - 98:7 **ٱلصَّٰلِحَٰتِ** ص ل ح B001: Soundness opposed to corruption unifies the practices as corrective work.
  - 98:5 **أُمِرُوٓا۟** ء م ر B002: Request and obligation frame the practices as a demanded order rather than optional sentiment.
  - 98:5 **لِيَعْبُدُوا۟** ع ب د B003: Submissive worship supplies the governing orientation of the practice.
  - 98:5 **مُخْلِصِينَ** خ ل ص B004: Exclusive separation from association makes the governing orientation undivided.
  - 98:5 **حُنَفَآءَ** ح ن ف B002: Inclination toward a direction makes the orientation active and chosen.
  - 98:5 **وَيُقِيمُوا۟ ٱلْقَيِّمَةِ** ق و م B004: Care, preservation, and charge make establishment a continuing work of maintenance.
  - 98:5 **ٱلصَّلَوٰةَ** ص ل و B003: Specified worship supplies the recurring embodied practice being maintained.
  - 98:5 **وَيُؤْتُوا۟** ء ت ي B002: Giving supplies the outward transfer that complements worship.
  - 98:5 **ٱلزَّكَوٰةَ** ز ك و B001: Growth and increase make the transfer productive rather than merely subtractive.

## mirrored response ranking
- reading: Within one created field, the verdict polarizes responses: truth covered into harm versus truth settled into beneficial repair.
- mechanism: The mirrored verdicts place concealment and evil opposite settled assent and beneficial excellence while retaining the same creation class. Khayr becomes a contrastive judgment on response, not free-floating superiority.
- trace:
  - 98:7 **ءَامَنُوا۟** ء م ن B002: Settled confirmation supplies the positive response opposed to covering truth.
  - 98:7 **ٱلصَّٰلِحَٰتِ** ص ل ح B001: Soundness opposed to corruption supplies the enacted positive pole.
  - 98:7 **خَيْرُ** خ ي ر B001: Beneficial good supplies the positive evaluative pole opposite evil.
  - 98:7 **ٱلْبَرِيَّةِ** ب ر ء B001: Created beings supply the comparison class shared by both verdicts.
  - 98:6 **كَفَرُوا۟** ك ف ر B003: Covering truth supplies the negative response opposed to settled confirmation.
  - 98:6 **شَرُّ** ش ر ر B001: Evil and harm supply the negative evaluative pole mirrored by khayr.
  - 98:6 **ٱلْبَرِيَّةِ** ب ر ء B001: The repeated creation branch confirms that both verdicts divide one creaturely field.

## rewarded reciprocity
- reading: Their excellence is a trajectory: reverent orientation becomes intentional repair and is completed by enduring provision and mutual satisfaction.
- mechanism: Recompense meets intentional work with nurturing completion, continuing provision, permanence, and mutual satisfaction, while reverent awe names the inward orientation. Excellence becomes a completed relationship rather than a static rank.
- trace:
  - 98:7 **وَعَمِلُوا۟** ع م ل B001: Intentional work supplies the human action answered in the following verse.
  - 98:7 **خَيْرُ** خ ي ر B005: Beneficence and gift open the predicate toward an answering generosity.
  - 98:8 **جَزَآؤُهُمْ** ج ز ي B001: Action met by its recompense supplies the response linking deed and outcome.
  - 98:8 **رَبِّهِمْ رَبَّهُۥ** ر ب ب B002: Nurture, repair, and completion make the relation developmental rather than merely transactional.
  - 98:8 **تَجْرِى** ج ر ي B006: Continuity of provision and giving turns the reward into sustained beneficence.
  - 98:8 **خَٰلِدِينَ** خ ل د B001: Stable persistence keeps the completed relation from quickly passing away.
  - 98:8 **رَّضِىَ وَرَضُوا۟** ر ض و B003: Mutual satisfaction supplies reciprocal ratification rather than one-sided approval.
  - 98:8 **خَشِىَ** خ ش ي B001: Fear with reverent awe supplies the inward orientation beneath the enacted response.

## release from entanglement
- reading: It can also register a post-disclosure transformation: assent and repair are the marks of release from corrupt entanglement.
- mechanism: The arrival of disclosed clarity activates an opening-and-disentangling image; together with corrective action and the clearance branch under bariyyah, this permits a transformation reading in which excellence is release from corrupt entanglement.
- trace:
  - 98:7 **ٱلصَّٰلِحَٰتِ** ص ل ح B001: Correction opposed to corruption supplies the condition into which release would move.
  - 98:7 **ٱلْبَرِيَّةِ** ب ر ء B002: Clearance and separation provide a form-distant echo inside bariyyah for disentanglement.
  - 98:1 **مُنفَكِّينَ** ف ك ك B001: Opening what is closed and separating what is entangled supply the release mechanism.
  - 98:1 **ٱلْبَيِّنَةُ** ب ي ن B004: Appearance and uncovering supply the clarity event at the mechanism's threshold.

## exchange valuation
- reading: As a contained analogy, intentional reparative work establishes one side of a value exchange answered by gift and fitting recompense.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 98:7 **وَعَمِلُوا۟** ع م ل B004: Wage for work supplies the possibility that action has a due return.
  - 98:7 **وَعَمِلُوا۟** ع م ل B005: Interpersonal transaction gives the work-return relation a social exchange form.
  - 98:7 **خَيْرُ** خ ي ر B005: Gift and beneficence recast khayr as generosity entering the exchange.
  - 98:6 **شَرُّ** ش ر ر B001: The non-dominant buying-and-selling branch activates the odd exchange frame from the mirrored verdict.
  - 98:8 **جَزَآؤُهُمْ** ج ز ي B001: Recompense matching action supplies the packet's explicit settlement of the exchange.

## convalescent creation
- reading: As a qualified root-echo, the best created response participates in making creaturely life sound, cleared, and restored.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 98:7 **ٱلصَّٰلِحَٰتِ** ص ل ح B001: Making sound against corruption supplies the reparative action.
  - 98:7 **ٱلْبَرِيَّةِ** ب ر ء B003: Recovery from illness supplies the form-distant convalescence image within the focus root.
  - 98:2 **مُّطَهَّرَةً** ط ه ر B001: Purity through removal of stain supports a clearing phase in the recovery image.
  - 98:5 **ٱلزَّكَوٰةَ** ز ك و B002: Purity and soundness connect prescribed giving to restored condition.
  - 98:8 **رَبِّهِمْ رَبَّهُۥ** ر ب ب B002: Repair, nurture, and completion extend recovery into a sustained creaturely process.


# Focus 98:8

## recompense as mutual acceptance
- reading: Their reward is a Lord-held settlement in which fitting recompense, habitation, divine acceptance, and their answering acceptance form one reciprocal outcome.
- mechanism: The focus begins with act-matching recompense but locates it in the recipients' Lord's presence and culminates in reciprocal consent. The gardens are therefore one component of a relationship brought into accepted alignment, not the whole content of the reward.
- trace:
  - 98:8 **جَزَآؤُهُمْ** ج ز ي B001: Matching an act with its recompense supplies the act-to-outcome correspondence that opens the model.
  - 98:8 **عِندَ** ع ن د B004: Proximity and presence place the recompense with their Lord rather than treating it as a detached asset.
  - 98:8 **رَبِّهِمْ رَبَّهُۥ** ر ب ب B002: Nurture and completion make the repeated Lord-relation the agency that brings the recipients and their outcome to completion.
  - 98:8 **رَّضِىَ وَرَضُوا۟** ر ض و B003: Reciprocal satisfaction supplies the two-way acceptance that turns recompense into relational settlement.

## garden as sustained habitat
- reading: The verse presents a protected, cultivated habitat whose hidden flow, growth, provision, and Lordly maintenance remain continuously operative.
- mechanism: The garden is a tree-covered, vigorously growing enclosure whose lower level carries opened watercourses and ongoing provision. Lordly nurture and the paired duration terms make it a continuously sustained habitat rather than a static scenic prize.
- trace:
  - 98:8 **جَنَّٰتُ** ج ن ن B003: The tree-covered enclosure gives the garden its sheltered spatial form.
  - 98:8 **جَنَّٰتُ** ج ن ن B011: Thick vigorous plant growth makes the enclosure productive and living rather than merely decorative.
  - 98:8 **تَجْرِى** ج ر ي B006: Ongoing provision turns the grammatical flow into the habitat's continuing supply mechanism.
  - 98:8 **تَحْتِهَا** ت ح ت B001: The literal lower side locates the sustaining movement beneath the inhabited garden.
  - 98:8 **ٱلْأَنْهَٰرُ** ن ه ر B003: Opening and widening until flow can pass supplies the channels that keep the garden watered.
  - 98:8 **رَبِّهِمْ رَبَّهُۥ** ر ب ب B002: Stagewise nurture and completion assign maintenance of this living system to the Lord-relation.
  - 98:8 **خَٰلِدِينَ** خ ل د B001: Enduring permanence keeps the supplied habitat from running down or passing away.
  - 98:8 **أَبَدًا** ء ب د B001: Emphatic duration extends the maintained condition without a terminal horizon.

## khashya as informed awe
- reading: The reward belongs to one whose apprehension of the Lord's authority has become reverent, responsive awe.
- mechanism: The closing qualification can hold knowledge and reverent fear together: apprehension of the Lord's authority and nurture becomes awe, so eligibility is a knowing orientation rather than raw fright.
- trace:
  - 98:8 **خَشِىَ** خ ش ي B002: The figurative knowledge branch supplies cognitive apprehension to the closing qualification.
  - 98:8 **خَشِىَ** خ ش ي B001: Fear joined with awe converts apprehension into a reverent affective stance.
  - 98:8 **رَبِّهِمْ رَبَّهُۥ** ر ب ب B001: Lordship and authority identify what is known and revered, anchoring the stance in ربه.

## evidence seals attachment
- reading: Abiding also reads as a definitive, disclosed-evidence-tested attachment in the Lord's presence.
- mechanism: The opening ayah holds unbinding in suspension until disclosure arrives. Against that release axis, the focus ayah's clinging permanence and presence with the Lord read as a settled attachment reached on the far side of disclosed evidence.
- trace:
  - 98:8 **خَٰلِدِينَ** خ ل د B002: Clinging and staying close supply the focus state that the context's unbinding axis recasts as settled attachment.
  - 98:8 **عِندَ** ع ن د B004: Presence at the Lord gives that settled attachment a relational location.
  - 98:1 **مُنفَكِّينَ** ف ك ك B001: Opening what is closed and separating what is entangled supplies the release pole against which focus permanence is reread.
  - 98:1 **ٱلْبَيِّنَةُ** ب ي ن B004: Emergence and disclosure make the bayyina the contextual threshold after which attachment can be evaluated.

## purified record becomes verdict
- reading: The gardens and reciprocal approval read as the openly executed settlement of an uncontaminated, upright record.
- mechanism: Purified written surfaces lead into writings that can establish a binding determination and are described through uprightness. These cues activate the debt-settlement branch of جزاء, making the focus reward readable as execution of a clean, upright record rather than an unannounced favor.
- trace:
  - 98:8 **جَزَآؤُهُمْ** ج ز ي B003: Collecting or settling a due gives the focus recompense a dischargeable-account form.
  - 98:8 **عِندَ** ع ن د B004: Presence and rank at the Lord locate the settlement at its authoritative source.
  - 98:2 **صُحُفًا** ص ح ف B002: The written-sheet image supplies a public, readable carrier for what precedes the focus judgment.
  - 98:2 **مُّطَهَّرَةً** ط ه ر B001: Purity as removal of stain qualifies that carrier as uncontaminated.
  - 98:3 **كُتُبٌ** ك ت ب B003: Writing that establishes an obligating judgment supplies the record-to-verdict function.
  - 98:3 **قَيِّمَةٌ** ق و م B008: Straightness and equilibrium characterize the recorded determination as upright.

## information requires reverent response
- reading: The qualifier is recognition converted into reverent response; manifest information by itself can precede division and does not satisfy the focus condition.
- mechanism: The context places division after the clear disclosure has arrived. That sequence blocks a reduction of خشي to information possessed: its knowledge branch must remain joined to awe, because exposure to clarity can still issue in fragmentation rather than the focus ayah's qualifying response.
- trace:
  - 98:8 **خَشِىَ** خ ش ي B002: Knowing as a figurative sense supplies the informational component whose sufficiency the context tests.
  - 98:8 **خَشِىَ** خ ش ي B001: Reverent fear supplies the responsive orientation that distinguishes khashya from bare reception of evidence.
  - 98:4 **تَفَرَّقَ** ف ر ق B002: Scattering into parts supplies the adverse response that follows disclosure.
  - 98:4 **جَآءَتْهُمُ** ج ي ء B001: Arrival fixes the bayyina as already present before the division occurs.
  - 98:4 **ٱلْبَيِّنَةُ** ب ي ن B004: Disclosure and emergence establish that what arrived was manifest rather than hidden.

## account opens into ongoing provision
- reading: Recompense closes what is due yet opens it into continuing, increasing provision, so settlement does not exhaust the gift.
- mechanism: Accounting and valuation cues establish a due, while giving and increase redirect that due toward expansion. Joined to the focus branches of debt settlement and ongoing provision, the reward becomes an account whose closure opens into an inexhaustibly continuing grant.
- trace:
  - 98:8 **جَزَآؤُهُمْ** ج ز ي B003: Demanding and settling a debt supplies the closing of an account at the focus.
  - 98:8 **تَجْرِى** ج ر ي B006: Continuing provision prevents settlement from becoming a one-time exhausted payment.
  - 98:5 **ٱلدِّينَ دِينُ** د ي ن B002: Accounting and recompense supply the context's explicit ledger-like frame.
  - 98:5 **وَيُقِيمُوا۟ ٱلْقَيِّمَةِ** ق و م B010: Valuation and appraisal supply a measure by which the due can be assessed.
  - 98:5 **وَيُؤْتُوا۟** ء ت ي B002: Giving turns assessed obligation into an outward transfer.
  - 98:5 **ٱلزَّكَوٰةَ** ز ك و B001: Growth and increase make the transfer productive rather than merely subtractive.

## permanence is neutral attachment
- reading: Eternal duration fixes attachment; the reward lies in what one is fixed within and in the reciprocal acceptance that characterizes that condition.
- mechanism: The same abiding construction attaches to fire in 98:6 and gardens in 98:8. Permanence is therefore not independently the reward; it intensifies whatever habitat and relation holds the person, while garden, provision, and reciprocal approval make the focus condition desirable.
- trace:
  - 98:8 **جَنَّٰتُ** ج ن ن B003: The tree-covered garden supplies the positive habitat to which focus permanence attaches.
  - 98:8 **خَٰلِدِينَ** خ ل د B001: Enduring permanence supplies a duration shared with the opposing contextual outcome.
  - 98:8 **رَّضِىَ وَرَضُوا۟** ر ض و B001: Acceptance opposed to displeasure gives the focus permanence its positive relational quality.
  - 98:6 **نَارِ** ن و ر B002: Kindled fire supplies the opposing habitat in which the same duration construction appears.
  - 98:6 **خَٰلِدِينَ** خ ل د B001: Enduring permanence on the fire side proves that duration alone is not a positive value.

## reward as wage and gift
- reading: The outcome holds both registers: it fittingly answers intentional work yet arrives as a generous provision whose flow is not exhausted by equivalence.
- mechanism: The immediate context supplies both the wage of intentional work and generosity or gift under خير. The focus matching-recompense branch supports earned correspondence, while its continuing provision exceeds a closed wage, preserving reward as both fitted return and generous grant.
- trace:
  - 98:8 **جَزَآؤُهُمْ** ج ز ي B001: Act-matching recompense supplies the earned-correspondence side of the reward.
  - 98:8 **تَجْرِى** ج ر ي B006: Ongoing grant supplies an excess beyond a wage that would be paid and exhausted.
  - 98:7 **وَعَمِلُوا۟** ع م ل B004: The wage or provision of a worker makes intentional deeds legible in an earned-payment register.
  - 98:7 **خَيْرُ** خ ي ر B005: Generosity and gift activate a simultaneous non-wage register for the same focus outcome.

## repair culminates in mutual settling
- reading: Approval is the completion of a repaired relation: estrangement has yielded to mutual consent, and the beneficiaries' inward state has settled with it.
- mechanism: Inward security and the removal of estrangement in 98:7 feed directly into the focus ayah's reciprocal satisfaction and settled inward state. Reward can thus be read as repaired relation becoming mutually acceptable and internally at rest.
- trace:
  - 98:8 **رَّضِىَ وَرَضُوا۟** ر ض و B003: Mutual consent supplies the reciprocal relational endpoint of the repair.
  - 98:8 **خَٰلِدِينَ** خ ل د B004: A thought settled in the heart gives the enduring outcome an inward resting dimension.
  - 98:7 **ءَامَنُوا۟** ء م ن B001: Security and trust that quiet the heart supply the inward condition leading toward settled satisfaction.
  - 98:7 **ٱلصَّٰلِحَٰتِ** ص ل ح B002: Reconciliation that removes estrangement supplies the relational repair completed by mutual رضا.

## reward as harvest at ripeness
- reading: Recompense resembles a harvest at ripeness: Lordly cultivation reaches completion and its mature yield becomes the garden outcome.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 98:8 **جَزَآؤُهُمْ** ج ز ي B002: Readiness for cutting or harvest supplies a form-distant image of recompense arriving when cultivated growth has matured.
  - 98:8 **جَنَّٰتُ** ج ن ن B011: Vigorous intertwined growth gives the harvest echo a concrete focus-domain substrate.
  - 98:8 **رَبِّهِمْ رَبَّهُۥ** ر ب ب B002: Stagewise nurture and completion provide the developmental path by which growth reaches readiness.
